				desc = bc_knight_the_wood_takes_them_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# In the green. The rarest band that is not dead - 8 of 137 - and therefore the
# only one of the three that needs no second row.
#
# THE HEAT IS THE ENEMY AND THAT IS THE POINT. bc_the_green_closes_in is the
# commander's jungle case and says the same thing about an army; this says it
# about one man in mail who has been upright in it since dawn.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_in_the_green = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_field_is_jungle_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Weight of harness and weight of years, in the one place where both
		# are paid for by the hour.
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			age >= 45
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_in_the_green"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_in_the_green_toast
				desc = bc_knight_in_the_green_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# BATCH 2026-08-17, THE SEVENTH ON THIS SIDE: THE BODY HE BROUGHT
#
# TWENTY-ONE TEMPLATES AND NOT ONE ASKS WHAT SHAPE THE MAN IS IN. The knight
# side reads prowess eleven times, age twice and temperament three times, and
# prowess is a number about how well he fights, not about what he is fighting
# with. A knight who rode in already carrying last summer's wound is the same
# 12 prowess he was before it.
#
# THE HEALTH TRAITS ARE THE ONE AXIS THE WHOLE MOD HAS NEVER READ - not here,
# not on the commander side, not in the eve. `wounded_1/2/3`, `maimed`,
# `one_legged`, `one_eyed` and `disfigured` were each confirmed present in
# AGOT's own common/traits/00_traits.txt, which OVERRIDES vanilla's wholesale,
# so a key that exists in the base game is not evidence on its own.
#
# THEY ARE COMMONER ON A BATTLEFIELD THAN ANYWHERE ELSE IN THE GAME, and that
# is what makes this worth three templates rather than a `chance` row: vanilla
# hands out `wounded_*` and `maimed` from combat itself, so the population this
# batch reads is the population that has already been in one of these battles.
# The mod's own moments are part of what puts them there.
#
# NONE OF THE THREE IS A SUBSET of a shipped gate, and the argument is short
# for once: no shipped `is_valid` on either side contains a health trait at
# all, so no health gate can be a subset of one. The side rows are what have
# to be checked, and they are: wounded + losing sits beside holds_the_breach
# (prowess >= 10), shames_himself (craven or prowess <= 8), walks_them_back
# (patient) and is_ridden_down (open ground), and a wounded knight of prowess
# 9 who is neither craven nor patient on rough ground matches none of them.
# The maimed case takes no side row at all, and the disfigured case pairs with
# winning, where the shipped gates are prowess >= 12, prowess >= 8 plus
# outnumbering, greedy, and forest.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The old wound. He has been fighting on it since the lines closed and it has
# stopped holding.
#
# THE GATE IS THE THREE `wounded_*` TRAITS AS ONE, not the worst of them. They
# are vanilla's severity ladder and a man is only ever on one rung of it, so
# an OR is the whole population; the rung is a `chance` row instead, which is
# where a difference of degree belongs.
#
# LOSING IS THE SECOND ROW AND IT IS ALSO THE CASE. A wound that opens while
# the side is winning is a detail. The same wound while the line is going
# backwards is the reason a piece of it goes backwards faster.
#
# `bloodied`/`down` AND A GUARDED FALTERING RUNG. bc_knight_in_the_green
# reaches the same cell from a different gate, which is the point of having a
# table: two unrelated moments that mean the same thing about the battle get
# the same consequence without either of them naming a modifier.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_old_wound = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		OR = {
			has_trait = wounded_1
			has_trait = wounded_2
			has_trait = wounded_3
		}
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# The top rung of the ladder the gate flattened.
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			has_trait = wounded_3
		}
		# A good enough man works around it for longer than this.
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			prowess >= 14
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_old_wound"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_the_old_wound_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_old_wound_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The man who cannot be carried off. `maimed` or `one_legged`: he has already
# paid a piece of himself for one of these days and he is standing in another.
#
# NO SIDE ROW, DELIBERATELY. Every other template in this batch takes one, and
# this is the case that would be spoiled by it: a man who is not going
# anywhere is the same man whether the line is going forward or back. Putting
# `losing` on it would turn it into a second bc_knight_the_old_wound.
#
# `entrenched`/`up` AND NO RUNG. bc_shieldwall on the side is the right
# consequence - a piece of the line that has stopped trying to manoeuvre is
# harder to move and slower to do anything - and a rung would say one man's
# footing decided the battle, which is the same call bc_knight_on_the_broken_
# ground made from the other direction.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_will_not_be_carried = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		OR = {
			has_trait = maimed
			has_trait = one_legged
			# AGOT SHIPS ITS OWN MAIMING TRAIT AND THIS OR NEVER SAW IT. `one_handed`
			# (00_agot_traits.txt:1411) is category health with prowess -15 and is what
			# AGOT hands a man who loses a hand - vanilla's `maimed` is not what its
			# events grant. Every such knight fell through this gate silently.
			has_trait = one_handed
		}
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# He was refusing to be moved before he had a reason.
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_will_not_be_carried"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes
			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_will_not_be_carried_toast
				desc = bc_knight_will_not_be_carried_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The face. `one_eyed` or `disfigured`, and the side is already winning.
#
# THE MOMENT IS NOT ABOUT HIS FIGHTING, which is what separates it from every
# prowess-gated case on this side. The men in front of him have decided
# something about him before he reaches them, and what he does with that is
# arrive somewhere they are not.
#
# WINNING IS THE ROW BECAUSE THE MOMENT NEEDS SOMEBODY ALREADY GIVING WAY. Men
# do not decline a fight they are winning; the same face on a losing field
# frightens nobody, and there is a shipped template about what a losing field
# does to a man's reputation.
#
# `ordered`/`up` AND A GUARDED MOMENTUM RUNG. The table maps the pair to
# bc_pursuit, which is exactly what a foot rank that would rather be elsewhere
# hands the side. The guard sits INSIDE the commander block because
# bc_has_momentum_trigger reads its own root and this is a knight event.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_face_they_run_from = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		OR = {
			has_trait = one_eyed
			has_trait = disfigured
			# THE TWO AGOT FACES THIS TEMPLATE WAS ALWAYS ABOUT AND NEVER READ.
			# `burned` (00_agot_traits.txt:1521) and `greyscale` (:1494) are AGOT's
			# own, and the mod's most famous candidate for this moment carries the
			# first of them rather than vanilla's `disfigured`.
			has_trait = burned
			has_trait = greyscale
		}
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# The face is only half of it. The other half is what he has done with
		# the years since he got it.
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_face_they_run_from"

		add_prestige = 25

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_the_face_they_run_from_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_face_they_run_from_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# BATCH 2026-08-17, THE EIGHTH ON THIS SIDE: ONE TEMPLATE, AND IT LEAVES
# SOMETHING BEHIND.
#
# TWENTY-FOUR KNIGHT AND COMMANDER TEMPLATES HAVE SHIPPED SINCE PLAN 1 AND NOT
# ONE OF THEM LEAVES ANYTHING PERMANENT. bc_shieldbrother_forged is still the
# only phase event in the mod that writes a memory and an opinion, and the only
# other permanent thing anywhere is bc_nemesis_memory, which is written from the
# aftermath rather than from a moment. Content batches are cheap; this is the
# expensive kind and it is worth one template rather than three.
#
# IT RUNS BETWEEN A KNIGHT AND HIS COMMANDER, which is the relation the mod has
# never touched. bc_shieldbrother_forged pairs two knights and the rivalry
# ladder pairs two commanders. The vertical one - the man in the ranks and the
# man whose day it is - had a toast at most.
#
# IT IS NOT A SUBSET OF bc_knight_holds_the_breach, which is the nearest gate
# (prowess >= 10, losing). This wants prowess >= 12, losing, AND a living
# commander who is not the knight himself, so it is strictly narrower - and that
# is the right direction: a narrower gate cannot steal a roll from the wider one
# it sits under any more often than it fires, and what it does with a roll is
# permanent.
#
# base = 60, NOT 80. It is the rarest thing on this side and the only one that
# writes a memory, and bc_shieldbrother_forged - the other template that leaves
# something behind - sits at exactly 60 too.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The knight who goes in after his lord.
#
# THE COMMANDER MUST EXIST AND MUST NOT BE THE KNIGHT. Both halves are load
# bearing. `?=` handles the leaderless side everywhere else in this file, but
# here the commander is not just the recipient of a toast - he is the man the
# memory is about, so a missing one makes the whole moment meaningless rather
# than merely unaddressed. And nothing in the API settles whether
# every_side_knight can return the side commander, so the identity test is the
# same guard bc_chronicles.0091 puts on its memory handout.
#
# THE GUARD IS WRITTEN IN is_valid AND NOT IN THE EFFECT, unlike the momentum
# guards this file's earlier batches carry. Those have to be in the effect
# because they read the COMMANDER's modifiers and is_valid is rooted on the
# knight; this one reads a link off scope:combat_side, which is available in
# both places, and a gate that decides whether the moment happens at all
# belongs in is_valid.
#
# NO RUNG, DELIBERATELY, AND THE MOMENT IS STILL `major`. The tier is what the
# ledger records about the day; the rung is what the army feels for the next
# twenty days. A commander being dragged out of a press does not make his line
# fight better - it is a thing that happened to him - and `adapted`/`up` maps
# to no modifier at all, which is the honest answer.
#
# THE OPINION RUNS FROM THE COMMANDER TOWARD THE KNIGHT, so it is written with
# add_opinion from inside the commander's scope rather than reverse_add_opinion
# from the knight's. bc_knight_shames_himself writes the mirror image of this
# and does it the other way round, from the knight's scope, because there the
# knight is root; both are correct and neither is a template for the other.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_pulls_him_out = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		prowess >= 12
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			NOT = { this = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			scope:combat_side.side_commander = { is_alive = yes }
		}
	}

	chance = {
		base = 60
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man who was already going to do something stupid.
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			has_trait = brave
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_pulls_him_out"

		# THE FIFTH POP-UP BRANCH, 2026-08-17, AND THE FIRST ON THIS SIDE OF
		# THE MOD. Four pop-ups have shipped and all four ask a COMMANDER
		# something; a player who is somebody's sworn sword has never once been
		# asked anything by this mod, which in AGOT is not an edge case.
		#
		# bc_battle_open_effect IS HOISTED OUT OF THE BRANCH AND RUN BEFORE THE
		# RAISE. It is the only way the commander crosses the trigger_event
		# boundary: scope:combat_side does not, but a scope SAVED before the
		# raise does, and this effect's first line saves the commander as
		# scope:bc_chronicle_subject. That is also the scope the pop-up's three
		# Custom clauses hang off, so hoisting buys the ground clause too.
		# bc_eve_offer_effect writes scope:bc_eve_foe one line before its own
		# raise for exactly this reason; this is the same trick.
		#
		# IT IS STILL CALLED EXACTLY ONCE PER EXECUTION, which the effect's own
		# header requires - both arms of the branch are downstream of it and
		# neither calls it again.
		#
		# THE `else` ARM IS THE UNCHANGED ORIGINAL, down to the prestige. Every
		# AI knight and every player inside the 60-day pop-up cooldown gets the
		# memory, the opinion and the toast this template has always given.
		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes
		}

		if = {
			limit = { bc_can_interrupt_player_trigger = yes }
			bc_start_popup_cooldown_effect = yes
			trigger_event = bc_chronicles.0005
		}
		else = {
			add_prestige = 100

			scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
				add_opinion = {
					target = scope:bc_knight
					modifier = bc_lifesaver_opinion
					opinion = 40
				}

				# The tag `lifesaver` must match the participants list in
				# bc_memories.txt; it is what the loc reads back with
				# [lifesaver.GetFullName].
				create_character_memory = {
					type = bc_lifesaver_memory
					participants = {
						lifesaver = scope:bc_knight
					}
				}
				debug_log = "BC: lifesaver memory"

				bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = major KIND = adapted SWING = up }

				send_interface_toast = {
					type = event_toast_effect_good
					title = bc_knight_pulls_him_out_toast
					desc = bc_knight_pulls_him_out_toast_desc
					left_icon = scope:bc_knight
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# BATCH 2026-08-17, THE NINTH ON THIS SIDE: THE TWO MEN WHO MATTER TO HIM
#
# `has_relation_*` APPEARS IN NO GATE ANYWHERE IN THE MOD. The rivalry ladder
# WRITES relations (`set_relation_nemesis` in bc_rivalry_effects.txt) and
# bc_side_has_kin_knight_trigger reads blood, but no `is_valid` in either phase
# event file has ever asked whether two men on the field already have a history
# with each other. Twenty-two knight templates ask what he is, how good he is,
# what shape he is in and what the battle is doing, and not one asks who he is
# standing near.
#
# THE AXIS IS DELIBERATELY THE KNIGHT'S, NOT THE COMMANDER'S. The commander
# side got the vertical relation in this session's station batch, from the top
# down: bc_his_blood_in_the_ranks is a commander noticing a knight. These three
# are the same field read from underneath, which is a different day.
#
# THE THIRD ONE READS THE OTHER SIDE. `scope:combat_side.enemy_side.
# side_commander` is the link bc_foe_is_heathen_trigger already uses, but every
# shipped use of it is rooted on a COMMANDER. A knight has never once been
# allowed to notice who is in command opposite, and a rival across a field is
# the most obvious reason he would.
#
# NONE OF THE THREE IS A SUBSET of a shipped gate, and the relation rows make
# it easy: no shipped `is_valid` reads a relation at all. The first pairs with
# nothing, the second with nothing, the third with winning - where the shipped
# knight gates are bc_knight_the_face_they_run_from (one_eyed/disfigured) and
# the ground and battle batches, none of which can ask about a relation.
#
# ALL THREE GUARD THE COMMANDER LINK THE WAY bc_knight_pulls_him_out DOES -
# `exists` first, then everything that reads it inside a `trigger_if` on the
# same `exists`, because CK3 does not short-circuit. The identity test
# (`NOT = { this = ... }`) is only needed on the two that read the OWN side's
# commander; a knight cannot be the enemy side's commander.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He is fighting where his friend can see him, and it is making a difference
# to how he fights.
#
# NO SIDE ROW. The moment is true on a winning field and a losing one, and it
# is about the man rather than about the day - bc_knight_will_not_be_carried is
# the shipped precedent on this side for a gate with no second row.
#
# `bloodied`/`up` AND A GUARDED MOMENTUM RUNG. He buys ground and pays the
# usual price for it; bc_bloodied is the modifier that means exactly that, and
# the guard sits INSIDE the commander block because bc_has_momentum_trigger
# reads its own root and `is_valid` here is rooted on the knight.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_for_a_friend = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			NOT = { this = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			has_relation_friend = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		}
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_for_a_friend"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_for_a_friend_toast
				desc = bc_knight_for_a_friend_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The other half of the vertical relation. He is here, he is armed, and he is
# doing precisely what he was told and not one thing more.
#
# THIS IS NOT COWARDICE AND THE PROSE MUST NOT MAKE IT ONE. bc_knight_shames_
# himself is the mod's craven case and it already owns that ground. A rival who
# holds his exact place is doing his duty in a way nobody can put a name to,
# which is a different and worse problem for the man giving the orders.
#
# `ordered`/`down` AND A GUARDED FALTERING RUNG - the formation keeps its shape
# and goes nowhere with it, which is the cell bc_knight_walks_them_back opened
# and the only KIND/SWING pair in the table whose `else` arm hands out nothing
# by design.
#
# NO PRESTIGE EITHER WAY. He has not disgraced himself and he has not done
# anything, and the ledger should say so by staying quiet.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_owes_him_nothing = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			NOT = { this = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			has_relation_rival = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		}
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man who was keeping score anyway.
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			has_trait = vengeful
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = forgiving
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_owes_him_nothing"

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_owes_him_nothing_toast
				desc = bc_knight_owes_him_nothing_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The first time a knight is allowed to notice who is in command opposite.
#
# NO IDENTITY TEST NEEDED. A knight of this side cannot be the enemy side's
# commander, so the `exists` and the `trigger_if` on it are the whole guard -
# unlike the two above, which both have to rule out the knight being his own
# side's commander.
#
# WINNING IS THE SECOND ROW BECAUSE THE MOMENT NEEDS ROOM. Going at the man
# commanding the other army is something a knight does when there is a way
# forward to do it in; on a collapsing side it is not a charge, it is a man
# being carried backwards past somebody he hates.
#
# `wild`/`up` AND NO RUNG. He gets there, or gets close enough that the field
# talks about it, and the horses pay for the getting - bc_spent_horse is the
# modifier and it is a cost, not a reward. The side's standing does not move,
# because one knight going hunting is not a turn in the battle; that is why the
# tier is `minor` and no rung is granted.
#
# `add_stress = -10` IS THE MOD'S FIRST NEGATIVE STRESS AND IT IS LEGAL. The
# 1.19.0.6 effect dump documents add_stress as "increases (or decreases) stress
# of a character" and vanilla writes the negative form at events/dlc/fp1/
# fp1_major_decision_events.txt:1404. This is NOT the `add_gold = -N` trap:
# that one throws a script error and has to be written as
# remove_short_term_gold, and the difference was checked rather than assumed.
# He has wanted this for years and he has just had some of it.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_knows_that_man = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.enemy_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.enemy_side.side_commander }
			has_relation_rival = scope:combat_side.enemy_side.side_commander
		}
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			has_trait = vengeful
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_knows_that_man"

		add_prestige = 50
		add_stress = -10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_knows_that_man_toast
				desc = bc_knight_knows_that_man_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# BATCH 2026-08-17, THE TENTH ON THIS SIDE: WHAT HE IS ALREADY CARRYING
#
# THE KNIGHT HALF OF THE STRESS BATCH. The argument is in the commander file's
# batch header and is not repeated: the mod writes stress thirty-four times and
# had never read it back, and stress is not temperament.
#
# ONE TEMPLATE HERE AND TWO THERE, because a knight has only one answer to
# this. A commander at the second breaking point can be misreading a battle or
# refusing to stop winning one - both are things a man does with an army. A
# knight has no army. He has the next few feet in front of him, and the only
# thing stress changes is how he goes at them.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The one thing out here that is quieter than the rest of his life.
#
# THE FIGHTING IS THE RELIEF AND THAT IS THE WHOLE TEMPLATE. He is at the
# second breaking point everywhere else and in the middle of this he is not
# thinking about any of it, because there is no room to. It is not courage and
# the prose may not write it as courage.
#
# `add_stress = -15` IS THE SECOND NEGATIVE STRESS IN THE MOD and the largest.
# The precedent and the reason it is not the `add_gold = -N` trap are recorded
# on bc_knight_knows_that_man above. Fifteen against the ten the pop-up options
# hand out, because this is the only place in the mod where the battle itself
# is the thing doing the mending.
#
# `bloodied`/`up` AND NO RUNG. He buys ground at the usual price. One knight
# fighting past himself does not turn a battle, so the side's standing does not
# move and the tier is minor - the same reasoning bc_knight_knows_that_man
# records.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_quiet_place = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		stress_level >= 2
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			stress_level >= 3
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_quiet_place"

		add_stress = -15

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_the_quiet_place_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_quiet_place_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE HEDGE KNIGHT, 2026-08-17.
#
# `is_lowborn` APPEARED NOWHERE IN THE MOD. Twenty-five knight templates read
# prowess, temperament, the health traits, relations, stress, age, kin, the
# ground and the state of the battle, and not one of them asked whether the
# man in the knight list has a name behind him. In Westeros that is the single
# most legible thing about a man on a field: whether the device on his shield
# is his house's or one he painted himself.
#
# THE TRIGGER IS ENGINE, NOT AGOT. `is_lowborn` is documented in the 1.19.0.6
# dump as "Is the character a lowborn?", character scope, and AGOT overrides no
# such thing - it is a dynasty test, and AGOT's dynasties are dynasties.
#
# THIS IS NOT THE STATION AXIS AGAIN. bc_the_crown_on_the_field and
# bc_nothing_but_the_horse read `highest_held_title_tier` and `is_landed` on a
# COMMANDER - what he stands to lose. Birth is not tenure: a landless third son
# of a great house is highborn with nothing, and a hedge knight who won a keep
# is landed and still lowborn. Different question, different root.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The man with no house behind him, on the day the field is turning his way.
#
# WINNING, BECAUSE THIS IS THE ONE DAY IT COUNTS FOR HIM. A highborn knight
# who does well is a lord's son doing what lord's sons do and nothing about the
# ledger changes; a hedge knight who does well in front of a winning host is
# the entire mechanism by which such a man ever becomes anything. The side row
# is what makes the template about a career instead of about a birth.
#
# THE PRESTIGE IS THE POINT AND IT IS THE LARGEST A KNIGHT TEMPLATE HANDS OUT.
# 150 against the 50 and 100 elsewhere in this file, for the reason above: it
# is the only currency a man with no house has, and this is the only place he
# can be paid in it.
#
# `bloodied`/`up` AND NO RUNG. He fights the way a man fights when there is no
# name to fall back on. One knight does not move the side's standing - the rule
# bc_knight_knows_that_man records - so minor, and the tide stays where it is.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_no_house_behind_him = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		is_lowborn = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 14
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_no_house_behind_him"

		add_prestige = 150

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_no_house_behind_him_toast
				desc = bc_knight_no_house_behind_him_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The other man with his arms on his shield, and the reason he is fighting
# harder than anybody near him.
#
# THE COMMANDER'S VERSION OF THIS GATE IS A MAN DECLINING TO PRESS
# (bc_he_will_not_chase_them). This is the opposite reading of the same fact
# and both are true of Westeros: the head of a house can afford to be
# merciful to a cousin, and the cousin cannot afford to be anything but
# conspicuous. A knight sharing a name with the man commanding the other host
# is, by construction of bc_foe_is_house_trigger, from a branch - and a branch
# knight in front of the senior line has one afternoon in his life to settle
# what he is worth.
#
# NOT bc_knight_meets_his_cousin AGAIN. That template takes
# bc_foe_is_kin_trigger, which is close family; this takes the house gate,
# whose final `NOT` excludes close family outright. The two are disjoint and
# the trigger's header carries the argument.
#
# NO SIDE ROW. Winning or losing does not change what he is doing - the
# audience he is performing for is on the other side of the field either way,
# and a branch knight going too far forward on a losing day is the same story
# with a worse ending.
#
# `bloodied`/`up` AND NO RUNG. He buys ground at the going rate and one knight
# does not move the side's standing, which is the rule bc_knight_knows_that_man
# records.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_other_branch = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_foe_is_house_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = content
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_other_branch"

		add_prestige = 75

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_the_other_branch_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_other_branch_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The man out here with a reason to want the day to end a particular way, and
# the reason is written down somewhere with his name on it.
#
# THE SAME `gold < 0` AS bc_the_chest_is_empty AND THE OPPOSITE SIDE ROW. A
# commander in debt on a losing field is watching the arithmetic arrive; a
# knight in debt on a WINNING field is looking at the only opportunity of its
# kind he is likely to get, because a beaten host leaves men on the ground who
# can be picked up and sold back to their families. The debt is the same fact
# and the day makes it two different templates.
#
# NOT bc_knight_takes_a_captive. That one takes `prowess >= 8`, winning AND
# outnumbering, and is about a host with the leisure to take prisoners. This
# takes neither prowess nor the numbers and asks a question about the man's
# ledger instead, so neither gate contains the other - both can match, and a
# knight who matches both is exactly the man the two are describing.
#
# NOT `greedy` EITHER. bc_knight_strips_the_dead is the temperament version
# and it robs the dead, which costs nothing and shames him. This man is not
# robbing anybody; he is working, and the mod's twenty-five gold rewards
# elsewhere are the precedent for what that pays.
#
# `bloodied`/`up` AND NO RUNG, the rule bc_knight_knows_that_man records.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_ransom_he_needs = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		gold < 0
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			gold < -100
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = greedy
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = generous
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_ransom_he_needs"

		add_gold = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_the_ransom_he_needs_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_ransom_he_needs_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# WHAT THE KNIGHT SEES ACROSS THE FIELD, BEYOND THE NAME, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE TWO FOE TRIGGERS HAD NEVER BEEN ASKED FROM A KNIGHT'S ROOT.
# bc_foe_is_heathen_trigger and bc_foe_is_countryman_trigger were written for
# the commander batch that read who is across the field, and both are rooted
# on the reader rather than on the side - they compare `root.faith` and
# `root.culture` against the enemy commander's, and root inside a
# `type = knight` phase event is the knight. So a knight asking them gets an
# answer about HIS faith and HIS culture, which is a different question from
# the one his commander would get, and in AGOT it very often has a different
# answer: a Reachman knight in a Northern host is not looking at the same men
# his lord is looking at.
#
# THE ENEMY COMMANDER STANDS IN FOR THE HOST, which is the same approximation
# the commander batch made and recorded. Nothing in the API gives a phase
# event the composition of the other side; the man leading it is the only
# handle there is, and both descs are written so that they are about what the
# man opposite MEANS rather than about a census of his army.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The one fight out here he has been told his whole life is worth having.
#
# `bloodied`/`up` AND NO RUNG, the rule bc_knight_knows_that_man records. He
# fights harder and it costs the usual thing; one knight does not turn a side.
#
# THE PIETY IS SMALL ON PURPOSE. 25 against the commander's 50 in
# bc_the_gods_are_watching, because a knight's conviction is between him and
# his gods and a commander's is a thing an army watches him do.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_against_unbelievers = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_foe_is_heathen_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			has_trait = zealous
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = cynical
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_against_unbelievers"

		add_piety = 25

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_against_unbelievers_toast
				desc = bc_knight_against_unbelievers_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The opposite reading of the same look across the field: they sound like
# home.
#
# NOT MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE WITH THE ONE ABOVE, and the trigger file says why -
# the two foe triggers each test one property, so a man of the same culture
# and a different faith answers yes to both. That is correct for gates: a
# phase event asks one question, and if both are true the roll picks one.
#
# `measured`/`down`, WHICH GRANTS NOTHING. He is not faltering and he is not
# refusing - he is doing the work with the volume turned down, and there is no
# army modifier for a man being careful. bc_knight_walks_them_back is the
# patient man who keeps his shape while going backwards; this is a man whose
# problem is who he is hitting rather than how it is going.
#
# NO SIDE ROW, because it is true whichever way the day is running, and the
# stress is the whole cost.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_they_sound_like_home = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_foe_is_countryman_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 20
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = sadistic
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_they_sound_like_home"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_neutral
				title = bc_knight_they_sound_like_home_toast
				desc = bc_knight_they_sound_like_home_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE SAME FACT FROM INSIDE THE RANKS, 2026-08-17.
#
# A KNIGHT CANNOT SEE A PLAN. He can see forty feet of shields and he can hear
# the horns, and that is the entire evidence available to him - which is why
# this is a separate template and not the commander's with a different icon.
# What reaches a man in the second rank about a good enemy commander is not
# "he has outthought us", it is that every single time the push goes in there
# is already something waiting there.
#
# THE GATE IS bc_foe_is_formidable_trigger, ASKED FROM A KNIGHT'S ROOT FOR THE
# FIRST TIME. Unlike the two foe triggers the knight file already borrows, this
# one reads nothing off `root` at all - it is entirely a statement about the
# other side - so its meaning is identical from either root. That is worth
# recording precisely because bc_foe_is_countryman_trigger and
# bc_foe_is_heathen_trigger are NOT like that, and a reader who has just come
# from those will assume this one shifts too.
#
# `measured`/`down`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING. The commander's version of this
# takes `adapted`/`down` because he is the one doing the reading; a knight is
# not adapting anything, he is being careful in a situation that keeps turning
# out to have been arranged. Neither cell grants a modifier, and there is
# nothing to grant: the men are doing what they were told and it keeps not
# working.
#
# THE TWO CANNOT COMPETE. Commander and knight templates are rolled from
# separate pools against separate `_none` weights, so a battle satisfying both
# gates gets at most one of each and never a contest between them.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_something_is_always_there = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_foe_is_formidable_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_something_is_always_there"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_something_is_always_there_toast
				desc = bc_knight_something_is_always_there_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE NAME ON THE OTHER BANNER, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE KNIGHT HALF OF THE NICKNAME BATCH, AND IT LOOKS THE OTHER WAY. The
# commander file asks whether OUR man has a name; from inside the ranks the
# interesting one is whose banner is over there, because a knight has spent
# his whole career hearing about men he has never seen and one of them is now
# four hundred yards away and losing.
#
# THE GATE IS INLINE, NOT A SHARED TRIGGER. `has_any_nickname` on the enemy
# commander is used once, and bc_scripted_triggers.txt is for gates that are
# shared or that need an argument recorded - a one-line read behind its own
# `exists` does not earn a name. The guard is written the same way
# bc_knight_knows_that_man writes its `has_relation_rival` read, for the same
# reason: CK3 does not short-circuit, so the `exists` and the read are
# separated by a `trigger_if` on that `exists`.
#
# WINNING IS THE WHOLE TEMPLATE. Losing to a famous man is the least
# surprising thing that can happen to a knight and there is no moment in it.
# Pushing him back is a thing he will be telling people about for thirty
# years, which is why the prestige is 100 - second only to the hedge knight's
# 150, and above every other knight award in the file.
#
# `bloodied`/`up`: bc_bloodied AND a momentum rung. The advantage is real and
# it is being bought at the front of a push against the best men the other
# side has, because a man with a name keeps good company. Nothing here is
# cheap.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_name_across_the_field = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.enemy_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.enemy_side.side_commander }
			scope:combat_side.enemy_side.side_commander = { has_any_nickname = yes }
		}
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_name_across_the_field"

		add_prestige = 100

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_the_name_across_the_field_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_name_across_the_field_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE KNIGHT HALF OF THE VERB-MODIFIER BATCH, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE ARGUMENT IS IN THE COMMANDER FILE'S BATCH HEADER and is not repeated:
# the four verb modifiers are written at every call site in the mod and read
# at none of them, and a gate on a live one is the first thing the mod has
# that can say "again".
#
# THE GATE REACHES THROUGH scope:combat_side.side_commander AND MUST. This
# file's tenth batch header already records why - the verb and rung families
# both live on the side commander, and `is_valid` here is rooted on the
# KNIGHT, so a bare `has_character_modifier = bc_pursuit` would ask whether
# this particular knight is personally carrying an army modifier, which he
# never is. That is not a hypothetical mistake; it is the exact mistake the
# momentum guards in this file were written to avoid.
#
# THE `exists` GUARD IS NOT OPTIONAL. CK3 does not short-circuit, so the read
# sits inside a `trigger_if` on its own `exists`, the same shape
# bc_knight_knows_that_man uses for its enemy-commander read.
#
# WHAT HE CAN ACTUALLY SEE. A knight cannot see a modifier and cannot see a
# ledger; what he can see is that the horses went past him a while ago and
# have not come back, and that he is now walking into a place they have
# already been. That is the entire content of the toast, and it is why this
# is a separate template rather than the commander's with a different icon.
#
# `wild`/`up`: bc_spent_horse and a momentum rung. The push is going in and it
# is going in behind cavalry that has been working since midday. It gains
# ground and it costs the horses, which is precisely the pair `wild` exists
# for.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_horses_went_past = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			scope:combat_side.side_commander = { has_character_modifier = bc_pursuit }
		}
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_horses_went_past"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_the_horses_went_past_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_horses_went_past_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE KNIGHT HALF OF THE LEDGER-AS-A-GATE BATCH, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE ARGUMENT IS IN THE COMMANDER FILE'S BATCH HEADER: the mod has counted
# its own moments since plan 1 and has never asked the count a question.
#
# `scope:combat_side.side_commander = { var:bc_battle_moments >= 4 }`, AND THE
# REACH-THROUGH IS MANDATORY. Every ledger variable is written on the side
# commander. A bare `var:bc_battle_moments` in this file would be read on the
# knight, where the variable has never existed and never will, so the gate
# would be silently always-false and the template would never fire once -
# bc_scripted_triggers.txt records exactly this trap on the shape of
# bc_chronicle_ready_trigger, where the same read is ALWAYS TRUE on a knight
# for the same reason.
#
# FOUR RATHER THAN THE COMMANDER'S THREE, and the extra rung is the point. A
# commander notices length at three because he is counting; a man in the ranks
# does not notice length, he notices that it has stopped being possible to
# tell how long it has been. Four is the ledger's own decisive threshold, and
# it makes this the rarer of the two.
#
# LOSING, WHICH THE COMMANDER'S VERSION DELIBERATELY IS NOT. Duration alone is
# not a side and the commander's template says so; but from inside the ranks
# duration and defeat are not separable - a long fight you are winning is a
# fight you are winning, and a long fight you are losing is the whole of the
# world.
#
# `bloodied`/`down`: bc_bloodied and the faltering rung. Nothing has been
# decided, and the price of nothing being decided is being paid by the men.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_he_has_lost_count = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			scope:combat_side.side_commander = { var:bc_battle_moments >= 4 }
		}
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			prowess <= 8
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_he_has_lost_count"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_he_has_lost_count_toast
				desc = bc_knight_he_has_lost_count_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE BOTTOM OF THE SAME LADDER, 2026-08-17, AND IT IS IN THIS FILE FOR A
# REASON.
#
# THE FALTERING HALF CANNOT BE A COMMANDER TEMPLATE. bc_rally_the_line's gate
# is `bc_chronicle_ready_commander_trigger` plus `bc_is_faltering_trigger`
# and nothing else, so any commander template gated on bc_faltering_decisive
# would be a STRICT SUBSET of a shipped one - every battle that could reach it
# can already reach rally, and the mod's distinctness rule exists to stop
# exactly that. Adding a side row would not help: the subset relation survives
# any row added on top.
#
# ON THIS SIDE THERE IS NO SUCH TEMPLATE. Not one knight `is_valid` in the
# file reads the rungs at all - every appearance of bc_is_faltering_trigger
# here is a `NOT` guard inside an effect - so the gate is free, and commander
# and knight pools roll separately in any case.
#
# THE READ IS THROUGH scope:combat_side.side_commander AND IT MUST BE, for the
# third time in three batches: the rung modifiers live on the commander and
# `is_valid` here is rooted on the knight. A bare
# `has_character_modifier = bc_faltering_decisive` would ask whether this
# particular knight is personally carrying an army rung, which he never is,
# and the template would never fire once.
#
# `wild`/`down`, AND NO RUNG GRANT AT ALL. The gate IS bc_faltering_decisive,
# so the usual `NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes }` guard could never
# pass here - writing the `if` would be dead code that reads like a live
# effect, which is worse than writing nothing. The host is already at the
# bottom of the ladder and there is no lower rung to hand it. bc_spent_horse
# is the whole mechanical content, and it is the right one: this is the state
# where things that were supposed to be somewhere else are running.
#
# FIFTEEN STRESS. The file's usual is ten; this is the mod's worst state and
# he is standing in it.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_bottom_of_it = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			scope:combat_side.side_commander = { has_character_modifier = bc_faltering_decisive }
		}
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_bottom_of_it"

		add_stress = 15

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_the_bottom_of_it_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_bottom_of_it_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The banner that is answerable for all of this, and he cannot get to it.
#
# THE SAME TRIGGER AS bc_the_man_who_started_it, POINTED THE OTHER WAY. The
# commander's version takes the winning row and is about an opportunity; this
# one takes the losing row and is about a distance. Neither is a subset of
# the other - the side rows are opposite - and they are rolled from separate
# pools anyway.
#
# THE TRIGGER READS NOTHING ABOUT THE MAN CALLING IT, which is why a knight
# may ask it at all. Its companion, bc_this_is_his_own_war_trigger, tests
# `this` against the side's war leader and would be permanently false here: a
# knight is never the war leader of his own side. That half of the batch is
# commander-only by construction, not by choice.
#
# `wild`/`down` AND bc_spent_horse. This is a man burning himself on ground he
# is not going to cross. The modifier is the correct consequence and the
# faltering rung is the correct rung, and the two together are the whole of

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The white cloak, and it does not matter which way this is going.
#
# NO SIDE ROW, AND THAT IS THE WHOLE CONTENT. Every other knight template in
# this file asks whether the day is going well before it says anything. This
# one does not, because the order's entire claim about itself is that the
# answer changes nothing about what he does, and a side row would be the
# template quietly disagreeing with that.
#
# `entrenched`/`up` AND bc_shieldwall FROM EITHER DIRECTION. The men near him
# form on the most visible thing on the field, which is him, and a line that
# has something to form on is braced whether it is going forward or not. The
# rung is guarded on momentum like the rest of the `up` grants.
#
# A HUNDRED PRESTIGE, WHICH IS THE FILE'S HIGH NUMBER SHORT OF THE HEDGE
# KNIGHT'S 150. Everything a man in that cloak does is watched and repeated,
# which is exactly what prestige measures, and it is the one award in this
# file that needs no argument about whether anybody saw it.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT PUT A KING ON THE FIELD. A Kingsguard is very often on
# detached service and the mod has no idea who he is guarding today; nothing
# available here can support it.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_white_cloak = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_is_kingsguard_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			prowess >= 14
		}
		# A cloak the singers already have a name for is watched harder.
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_any_nickname = yes
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_white_cloak"

		add_prestige = 100

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_the_white_cloak_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_white_cloak_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE BOY IN THE ARRAY, 2026-08-17.
#
# `squire` APPEARS ZERO TIMES ANYWHERE IN THE MOD. Thirty-nine knight
# templates ask how well he fights, what shape he is in, what he believes,
# who he is related to and what the day is doing, and none of them asks
# whether the man in the knight list has been a man for very long. In AGOT a
# squire in an array is ordinary and the trait is handed out constantly.
#
# THE TRAIT, NOT is_agot_knight_trigger. AGOT's knight trigger counts a squire
# with 100 track experience as a knight already, which is the right answer for
# knighting ceremonies and the wrong one here: the question is what he is
# today, in this line, and the trait answers it directly.
#
# TWO TEMPLATES, ONE GATE, OPPOSITE SIDE ROWS, and the pair is the point. The
# same boy has the best day of his life or finds out what he agreed to, and
# nothing about him decides which - only what the rest of the field is doing.
# The mod has one other pair built this way, on nicknames, and the argument is
# the same one.
#
# NO COMMANDER HALF. A squire does not hold a host. If AGOT ever produces one
# the gate would be reachable and still wrong.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The best day of his life, and nobody has told him to slow down.
#
# `wild`/`up` AND bc_spent_horse WITH A MOMENTUM RUNG. This is the cell for a
# moment that is genuinely good and genuinely costly in the same breath - the
# two shipped uses are bc_the_wrath_takes_him and bc_the_old_grudge - and a
# boy who has stopped being frightened is exactly that. He is riding it into
# the ground and it is working.
#
# TWENTY-FIVE PRESTIGE, THE FILE'S SMALLEST AWARD. He is not being knighted
# this afternoon and the mod may not imply it. What he has is that men who
# will be alive tomorrow saw him.
#
# PROWESS >= 10, NOT 12. The rest of the file uses 12 because it is asking
# about a knight. This is asking whether a boy is unusual for a boy.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_squires_best_day = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		has_trait = squire
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			prowess >= 10
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = brave
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_squires_best_day"

		add_prestige = 25

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_the_squires_best_day_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_squires_best_day_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He finds out what he agreed to.

# FIFTEEN STRESS, WHICH IS THE FILE'S HIGH NUMBER. The usual is ten and it is

# nothing about it is going to stop happening for several hours.
#
# `measured`/`down`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING, AND NOT `wild`/`down`. Wild
# would say he came apart, and the mod does not know that - what it knows is
# that a thing he had a picture of has been replaced by the thing itself. The
# faltering rung and the stress are the whole cost, and there is no army
# modifier for a boy's education.
