# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# There is nothing in him worth taking alive.
#
# THE LOSING HALF OF bc_knight_the_ransom_he_needs, AND THE MOD'S ONE PLACE
# WHERE `gold` IS ABOUT THE MAN AND NOT ABOUT HIS PURSE. On a winning field a
# broke knight looks at the other host and sees money. On a losing field the
# other host looks at him and sees none, and a man nobody will be paid for is
# a man there is no reason to take gently.
#
# `bloodied`/`down` AND bc_bloodied, WHICH IS THE RARE CASE WHERE THAT
# MODIFIER IS LITERALLY WHAT IS HAPPENING. bc_bloodied is men fighting harder
# and worse; this is a man who has understood that surrendering is not one of
# the things available to him this afternoon, which produces exactly that.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT KILL HIM OR WOUND HIM - the standing rule - AND MAY NOT
# SEND HIM RUNNING, which bc_knight_shames_himself owns. What it may say is
# what he has worked out and what he is doing about it.
#
# `craven` LOWERS IT RATHER THAN RAISING IT. A craven man in this position has
# a template of his own and it is the one about running.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_nobody_pays_for_him = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		gold < 0
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			gold < -100
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_nobody_pays_for_him"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_nobody_pays_for_him_toast
				desc = bc_knight_nobody_pays_for_him_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A good day is not enough for him and he knows the rate.
#
# `bastard` AND THE WINNING ROW, WHICH IS THE HALF THE COMMANDER FILE COULD
# NOT TAKE. The commander half of this batch is the losing one, because what
# happens to a bastard who loses is that nobody has to look for an
# explanation. What happens to a bastard who is winning is entirely different
# and it happens down in the array rather than at the top: the day is going
# well and he is still working at a rate nobody else in the line is working
# at, because a good day is not a thing he gets to keep unless it is
# undeniable.
#
# NOT bc_knight_no_house_behind_him. That one is `is_lowborn` - a man with
# nobody behind him, and being seen is the whole of his reward. This one has
# a house behind him that is not going to say so, and being seen is not the
# same currency at all. The two gates ask different questions and neither
# contains the other; the reasoning is in the commander file's batch header.
#
# NOT bc_knight_the_glory_hound EITHER, which is a man taking a risk for the
# story. This one is not taking a risk. He is being twice as careful and
# twice as long about it, which is a different failure mode and gets a
# different KIND.
#
# `bloodied`/`up` AND bc_bloodied. The rate is the moment, and the modifier -
# harder and worse - is what a whole line looks like when the man at its
# hinge will not let a thing be good enough.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE, and it is the one award in the file that is explicitly not
# a reward. He is paying for something with it.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_twice_as_good = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		has_trait = bastard
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = content
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_twice_as_good"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_twice_as_good_toast
				desc = bc_knight_twice_as_good_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ===========================================================================
# THE BODY HE WAS BORN WITH, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE MOD HAS A HEALTH BATCH AND HAS NEVER ASKED ABOUT BUILD. `wounded_*`,
# `maimed`, `one_eyed` and `one_legged` are all read, and all four are things
# that HAPPENED to a man. `physique_good_*` and `physique_bad_*` appear zero
# times, and they are the other question: not what the war did to him but what
# he was handed before any of it started.
#
# THE LADDERS ARE OR-ED AT THE TOP TWO RUNGS AND THE THIRD IS A `chance` ROW.
# Same shape as the `wounded_*` ladder in bc_knight_the_old_wound. Gating on
# the extreme rung alone would make both templates nearly unreachable - AGOT
# hands out physique_good_1 and physique_bad_1 constantly and the far ends
# rarely - and gating on all three would make "built for this" true of half
# the array, which is not a moment.
#
# NOT A SUBSET OF THE PROWESS TEMPLATES, AND THE DISTINCTION IS THE WHOLE
# POINT. Prowess is how well he fights. This is what he is carrying while he
# does it, and the mod has men who are good at this and built wrong for it,
# which is a more interesting afternoon than either alone.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He was built for this and it is the one day it pays.
#
# `entrenched`/`up` AND bc_shieldwall. This is not a man doing something
# clever or something brave - it is a man who is simply harder to move than
# the men on either side of him, and what a line does around a fixed point is
# exactly what that modifier means.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT MAKE HIM A GOOD FIGHTER. Prowess is a separate question and
# has its own templates; this one is about mass and reach and a long arm, and
# a knight of prowess 6 may fire it.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE. The men can see it from a distance, which is most of why it
# is worth anything.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_built_for_it = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		OR = {
			has_trait = physique_good_2
			has_trait = physique_good_3
		}
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			has_trait = physique_good_3
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			prowess >= 12
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_built_for_it"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_built_for_it_toast
				desc = bc_knight_built_for_it_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The harness weighs what it weighs.
#
# THE OTHER END OF THE SAME LADDER AND THE LOSING ROW. A battle is hours of
# carrying sixty pounds about, and the mod has never once said that this costs
# different men different amounts. It costs this one more, and it has been
# costing him since before the fighting started.
#
# `measured`/`down`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING. There is no army modifier for a
# man being tired. The faltering rung and the stress are the cost, and the
# reason it is faltering rather than nothing at all is that he is in the line
# and the line is where the tiredness goes.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT WOUND HIM, KILL HIM OR SEND HIM RUNNING - the standing
# rule, and bc_knight_shames_himself owns running. He is still there. That is
# the point of him.
#
# `stubborn` RAISES IT. The template is about a man staying in a place his
# body has stopped agreeing with, which is what the trait describes.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_it_weighs_the_same = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		OR = {
			has_trait = physique_bad_2
			has_trait = physique_bad_3
		}
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 40
			has_trait = physique_bad_3
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_it_weighs_the_same"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_it_weighs_the_same_toast
				desc = bc_knight_it_weighs_the_same_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The spurs they took.
#
# AGOT SHIPS A TRAIT FOR A MAN WHO WAS A KNIGHT AND IS NOT ONE NOW, AND
# NOTHING IN THIS MOD HAS EVER READ IT. `stripped_knight` (00_agot_traits.txt:154)
# is category fame, opposite_opinion -10, monthly_prestige_gain_mult -0.01, and
# opposites = { squire }. That last line is why this template cannot collide
# with batch forty-three's pair: a man cannot hold both traits, so the squire's
# best day and this are disjoint by AGOT's own definition rather than by
# anything written here.
#
# LOSING, BECAUSE WINNING WOULD BE A STORY ABOUT GETTING THEM BACK. Nobody
# gives spurs back in the middle of a rout, and a template that hinted they
# might would be writing a promise the mod cannot keep. He is in the line on
# the bad day, which is the only place the trait has anything to say.
#
# NO PRESTIGE, NOT EVEN A LITTLE. The trait's whole mechanical content is a
# prestige penalty. Handing him prestige here would be the mod arguing with
# the trait it just read, and the argument would be won by whichever ran last.
#
# NO RUNG AT ALL, WHICH IS DELIBERATE AND HAS PRECEDENT. bc_knight_will_not_be_carried
# grants none either. Faltering would be wrong twice over: the side is already
# losing, and this is a man doing the one thing he still can rather than a man
# coming apart. `entrenched`/`down` means bc_shieldwall is the entire
# consequence - the line holds a little longer where he is standing, and that
# is all anyone will ever be able to say about him.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT KNIGHT HIM, MAY NOT PROMISE TO, AND MAY NOT SEND HIM
# RUNNING - bc_knight_shames_himself owns running, and the other two would
# undo the trait.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_spurs_they_took = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		has_trait = stripped_knight
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_spurs_they_took"

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_the_spurs_they_took_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_spurs_they_took_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# THE WOUND THE MOD GAVE HIM THIS AFTERNOON, 2026-08-17.
#
# `bc_battle_wounded` IS SET BY bc_wound_effect ON THE COMMANDER AND HAS ONLY
# EVER BEEN READ BY bc_wound_effect ITSELF, as the once-per-battle guard that
# stops a second decisive-and-down moment wounding him twice. The mod puts a
# `wounded` trait on a commander in front of the player, sends a toast about
# it, and then for the rest of that battle behaves exactly as though it had
# not happened.
#
# THESE ARE KNIGHT TEMPLATES AND THAT IS THE WHOLE REASON THEY EXIST. The
# commander pool cannot have them: `bc_he_cannot_get_there` is already gated on
# `wounded_1/2/3` and losing, and a gate of "was wounded in THIS battle" is a
# strict subset of that one - the wound effect only fires when the man does not
# already hold the trait, so every commander carrying `bc_battle_wounded` also
# passes the `wounded_*` OR. The distinctness rule forbids that. From the
# knight list it is not a subset of anything: the two pools roll separately,
# and the fact being read is about somebody else entirely.
#
# THE VARIABLE IS SCRATCH AND THAT IS FINE HERE. bc_clear_scratch_effect
# removes it at combat end, so it means "wounded during the battle now being
# fought" and nothing else - which is precisely the reading these templates
# want, and the reason neither of them may say anything about old wounds.
# `bc_knight_the_old_wound` owns those, on the knight himself.
#
# NO NEW SCRIPTED TRIGGER. The walk is inline, the same shape
# `bc_knight_he_has_lost_count` uses for `var:bc_battle_moments`: `exists` on
# the scope as a sibling, the read nested in a trigger_if. `exists` on a
# missing variable is itself safe, so the inner row needs no further guard.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# They can see he is still up there.
#
# WINNING, AND THE ORDER OF EVENTS IS THE POINT. A wound only ever comes out
# of a decisive-or-major moment that went DOWN, in a battle big enough to
# qualify - so a knight reading this on a winning field is standing in a host
# that was hit hard enough to cost it its commander's blood and is winning
# anyway. That sequence is the moment. It cannot be written as a commander
# template because from the saddle it is not visible at all.
#
# `bloodied`/`up` AND bc_bloodied. Men who have watched the banner-holder take
# a hit and stay in the saddle do not fight more cleverly, they fight harder
# and the other side pays for it. That is the one modifier in the table that
# means exactly that.
#
# NO RUNG. One knight's view of the field does not move the side's standing -
# the rule bc_knight_knows_that_man records - and the wound moment that caused
# this already moved it downward on its own terms.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT HEAL HIM, MAY NOT GET HIM OFF THE FIELD AND MAY NOT SAY
# WHERE THE WOUND IS. The trait is `wounded` and nothing in the mod knows more
# than that; the recovery pulse owns how long it lasts.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_lord_is_still_up = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			scope:combat_side.side_commander = { exists = var:bc_battle_wounded }
		}
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = brave
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_lord_is_still_up"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_the_lord_is_still_up_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_lord_is_still_up_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The same fact, arriving down a line that is already going backwards.
#
# THE DIAGONAL, AND THE FACT DOES NOT CHANGE - ONLY WHO IS TELLING IT. A host
# that is winning watches its commander bleed; a host that is losing hears
# about it fourth-hand, and by the time the sentence reaches the fifth rank it
# has become something worse than what happened. Nothing in either template
# says the commander is doing anything differently, because he is not: the
# variable records a wound, not a collapse.
#
# `ordered`/`down`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING. This is the one place in the mod
# where that cell is exactly right rather than merely available: the orders
# are still correct and still arriving, and the men receiving them have
# stopped being certain who sent them. There is no army modifier for a chain
# of command that is intact and no longer believed, and inventing one would
# make this template say the line broke, which it must not.
#
# A GUARDED FALTERING RUNG AND TEN STRESS. The rung is on the commander, where
# every knight template's state writes go, and it is guarded because the wound
# moment may well have set it already.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT KILL HIM, MAY NOT DEPOSE HIM AND MAY NOT LET THE RUMOUR BE
# TRUE. bc_banner_falls owns the banner going down and bc_chronicles.0002 owns
# a line deciding what it is frightened of; this is the hour before either.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_word_came_down_the_line = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			scope:combat_side.side_commander = { exists = var:bc_battle_wounded }
		}
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_host_is_vast_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_word_came_down_the_line"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_word_came_down_the_line_toast
				desc = bc_knight_word_came_down_the_line_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The man who swore in front of a tree.
#
# THE THIRD RELIGION, IN THE OTHER POOL. `the_pact_religion` holds the old gods
# (00_agot_the_pact.txt:213). The two pools roll separately, so this is not the
# distinctness question at all - it is the fact asked of a man in the line
# rather than a man on a horse, which is where it belongs: what the old gods
# have on a battlefield is an absence of liturgy, and an absence is only
# audible from four feet away.
#
# NO FIELD ROW, DELIBERATELY. The obvious version of this template puts him in
# a wood, and `bc_field_is_forest_trigger` with a winning row is already
# `bc_knight_the_wood_takes_them`'s gate - adding a religion row to it would make
# this a strict subset of a shipped template, which the mod does not allow. It
# is also wrong on its own terms: a northman who is a long way from any
# godswood is the more interesting half of this, and he is the half nothing in
# the mod has ever mentioned.
#
# LOSING. The southron knight on a bad afternoon has a septon somewhere behind
# the line and a set of words to say; this man has neither, and the template is
# the difference between those two facts rather than anything about courage.
#
# `measured`/`down`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING. There is no army modifier for a
# man having nothing to say, and there should not be. The faltering rung and
# the stress are the whole cost, and the rung is guarded because the afternoon
# has probably set it already.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT PRAY ALOUD, MAY NOT PUT A TREE ON THE FIELD AND MAY NOT
# MAKE HIM DOUBT THE GODS. He is not having a crisis. He is having an ordinary
# bad hour with nothing to fill the silence with, which is the entire point.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_nothing_to_say_over_him = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		has_religion = religion:the_pact_religion
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			has_trait = zealous
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_nothing_to_say_over_him"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_nothing_to_say_over_him_toast
				desc = bc_knight_nothing_to_say_over_him_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE FALTERING HALF, IN THE POOL WHERE IT IS LEGAL. bc_rally_the_line owns
# `faltering` in the commander pool outright - its is_valid is the ready
# trigger and that one row - so the read comes here instead, walked out to the
# man on the horse. The two pools roll from different lists and never compete,
# and the fact being asked about is not even the same one: the rally is about
# the commander finding the moment, and this is about what the modifier looks
# like from forty yards back in the line.
#
# THE WALK IS bc_knight_word_came_down_the_line'S EXACTLY - `exists` as a
# sibling, the read nested in a trigger_if repeating it - because a combat
# side can exist without a commander. The character modifiers need no variable
# guard of their own; `has_character_modifier` is safe on any character scope.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_word_came_down_the_line. That one reads
# `var:bc_battle_wounded` - the commander was cut this afternoon, a fact about
# his body. This reads the mod's own verdict on how his battle is going, which
# can be true of a man who has not been touched all day. Neither gate implies
# the other in either direction.
#
# NO RUNG, AND THE GUARD IS WHY. The faltering grant everywhere in this mod is
# wrapped in `NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes }` and this template gates
# on the commander already carrying it, so the grant would be dead code. The
# stress on the knight is the whole cost.
#
# `entrenched`/`down` AND bc_shieldwall. Nothing is arriving from behind, so
# the line stops waiting for it and does the only thing a line can do on its
# own initiative, which is get closer together and stop moving. That is worth
# a modifier and it is worth exactly that one.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE COMMANDER'S CONDITION, may not have him seen at
# all, and may not have anybody decide to run. The knight does not know why
# the orders stopped; he knows they stopped.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_nothing_is_coming_from_behind = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			scope:combat_side.side_commander = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes }
		}
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_side_is_outnumbered_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_nothing_is_coming_from_behind"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_nothing_is_coming_from_behind_toast
				desc = bc_knight_nothing_is_coming_from_behind_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ===========================================================================
# WHAT THE MAN IN THE LINE THINKS OF THE MAN ON THE HORSE, 2026-08-17.
#
# THE MOD HAS NEVER READ AN OPINION VALUE. It reads relations - friend, rival,
# kin, liege - which are the categorical version of the same thing, and the
# engine's `opinion` trigger, sitting there since the base game, has not been
# touched in sixty-three batches. Relations are rare and legible; opinion is
# universal and quiet, and every knight on every field has one.
#
# BOTH TEMPLATES TAKE THE NEGATIVE HALF, AND THAT IS DELIBERATE RATHER THAN
# SOUR. The positive half is already occupied in feel by bc_knight_for_a_friend,
# which gates on `has_relation_friend` toward the same man and has NO side row
# at all - a high-opinion template would be the same afternoon written twice.
# A knight who cannot stand the man he is fighting for is not written anywhere
# in this mod and is the commoner case by a very long way.
#
# `opinion = { target = X value <= -25 }` IS READ ON THE KNIGHT, NOT ON THE
# COMMANDER. The trigger asks the SCOPED character's opinion of the target, so
# root is correct here and no scope change is needed - unlike the wound and
# faltering reads, which had to enter the commander's scope.
#
# -25 IS THE THRESHOLD BECAUSE IT IS THE POINT WHERE IT IS NO LONGER JUST
# COLDNESS. A single bad trait pairing or a stale grievance will put two men
# at -10 without either of them thinking about it; -25 takes a reason, and the
# templates below are written as though the knight could name his if asked.
#
# THE IDENTITY TEST IS REQUIRED. bc_knight_for_a_friend carries
# `NOT = { this = scope:combat_side.side_commander }` for exactly this reason -
# the same character can be both, and a man's opinion of himself is not what
# either of these is about.
# ===========================================================================

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE WINNING HALF. He has done his part of it properly, because doing it badly
# would cost him and not the man he dislikes, and the afternoon is going to be
# written down under a name that is not his.
#
# `measured`/`up`, WHICH HANDS OUT NOTHING. The army is not being changed by
# this - it is being served exactly as well as it would have been by a man who
# loved his commander. That is the point, and an army modifier would turn a
# private grievance into a battlefield effect it is not.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS STILL GRANTED, GUARDED, INSIDE THE COMMANDER BLOCK. The
# rung is a fact about the side and not a compliment to anybody; the side is
# winning and a knight has just done his job well, which is all it means.
# The guard is inside the block because bc_has_momentum_trigger reads its own
# root and this is_valid is rooted on the knight - bc_knight_for_a_friend's
# comment makes the same point.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT SAY WHY HE DISLIKES HIM. The gate is a number and knows no
# reason. It may not have him sabotage anything, refuse an order, or be seen
# resenting it out loud, because all three would be a different template.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_it_will_be_his_name = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			NOT = { this = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			opinion = { target = scope:combat_side.side_commander value <= -25 }
		}
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_it_will_be_his_name"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_it_will_be_his_name_toast
				desc = bc_knight_it_will_be_his_name_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE LOSING HALF, AND THE DIFFERENCE BETWEEN THEM IS INITIATIVE. On a winning
# field the grievance costs the army nothing; on a losing one it costs it the
# thing a bad hour actually runs on, which is men doing slightly more than they
# were told to without being asked.
#
# `measured`/`down`, ALSO HANDING OUT NOTHING, AND FOR THE OPPOSITE REASON. He
# is not failing. Every order he was given has been carried out to the letter
# and that is precisely the complaint - there is no army modifier for the
# absence of a thing nobody is owed, and inventing one would make this a
# template about a knight sabotaging a battle, which it is not.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED, GUARDED. The side is losing and this is one of
# the ways a losing side gets worse; the guard is inside the commander block
# for the same rooting reason as the winning half.
#
# NOT A SUBSET OF bc_knight_for_a_friend IN EITHER DIRECTION - that gate is a
# relation and has no side row, this is a number and has one - and not a subset
# of bc_knight_nothing_is_coming_from_behind, which asks about the commander's
# faltering modifier and can be true of a man everybody adores.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT HAVE HIM DISOBEY, RUN, OR SAY ANY OF THIS ALOUD. He is a
# professional having a bad afternoon in the service of a man he does not like,
# and the only thing missing is the part nobody can order.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_exactly_what_was_asked = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			NOT = { this = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			opinion = { target = scope:combat_side.side_commander value <= -25 }
		}
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_side_is_outnumbered_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = cynical
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_exactly_what_was_asked"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_exactly_what_was_asked_toast
				desc = bc_knight_exactly_what_was_asked_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE FREE FOLK, LOSING, AND IN THE OTHER POOL ON PURPOSE. The fact is about a
# man in a line rather than a man on a horse: a wildling host has a leader
# because enough individual men decided it had one, and the decision is made
# again every hour by every one of them. From the saddle that is invisible
# until it has already happened. From inside the line it is the loudest thing
# on the field.
#
# bc_he_is_free_folk_trigger IS A CULTURE READ, NOT A TRAIT READ. AGOT marks
# its wildling cultures with a variable and the wrapper asks the culture, so a
# kneeler raised beyond the Wall counts and a free folk man who took a southron
# seat does not stop counting - which is the right way round for a template
# about what a man expects an army to be.
#
# `ordered`/`down`, WHICH FALLS THROUGH THE VERB TABLE AND HANDS OUT NOTHING.
# That is the most deliberate choice in this batch. Nothing has broken: the
# line is still there, still fighting, still where it was. What is leaking is
# consent, and the mod refuses to model consent leaking as an army modifier,
# because the moment it does so the template becomes a prediction that these
# men will run - which is a thing the mod does not know and would be a slander
# on a host that beat the Watch more often than it lost to it.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS THE WHOLE COST, guarded, inside the commander block
# like every knight-pool grant, because the rung is a fact about the side.
#
# NOT A SUBSET OF bc_knight_exactly_what_was_asked. That one is a professional
# withholding the unasked-for extra from a man he dislikes; this is a man who
# owes nothing to begin with and likes the fellow well enough. Opinion is not
# read here and culture is not read there.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT HAVE HIM LEAVE, MAY NOT HAVE ANYBODY ELSE LEAVE, AND MAY
# NOT SNEER AT KNEELERS. He is still fighting when the toast ends. The whole
# moment is the arithmetic being available, not the sum being done.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_nobody_here_is_sworn = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_he_is_free_folk_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_side_is_outnumbered_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = fickle
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_nobody_here_is_sworn"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_nobody_here_is_sworn_toast
				desc = bc_knight_nobody_here_is_sworn_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE KNIGHT'S HALF, WINNING, AND IT IS ABOUT BEING WET. The commander's
# riverside template is geometry; this one is what the geometry feels like to
# the man it funnels. Winning at a crossing is done standing in the thing,
# on stones nobody can see, in armour that has stopped being an advantage.
#
# `bloodied`/`up` AND bc_bloodied, ON A WINNING SIDE. The mod's bloodied row
# goes to the same modifier in both directions and that is exactly right here:
# the side is ahead and the price is being paid at this one spot, by these men,
# at close range, because there is nowhere on a ford to fight at any other
# range.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED, GUARDED, INSIDE THE COMMANDER BLOCK - the rung
# is a fact about the side and this is a knight-rooted is_valid, the same
# rooting point bc_knight_for_a_friend's comment makes.
#
# NOT A SUBSET OF bc_knight_the_wood_takes_them OR ANY OTHER GROUND TEMPLATE.
# Those read the six bands; a riverside province can sit in any of them.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT DROWN ANYBODY, may not name the river, and may not have him
# cross it. He is in it, the fighting is in it, and the toast ends with him
# still there.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_water_is_over_his_boots = {

			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}

			factor = 2

			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {

			has_trait = brave

	effect = {

		add_prestige = 50

				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }

				type = event_toast_effect_good

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He leaves the horse in the shallows. Coastal, and the side is losing.
#
# THE SAME EDGE FROM THE OTHER END. The commander's coastal moment is a man
# counting a shorter line; this one is a man standing on the part of it that
# the sea is not helping. Wet sand carries a walking man and does not carry a
# destrier, and the fighting on a shoreline ends up at the waterline because
# that is the only direction anyone can be pushed.
#
# `adapted`/`down`, THE RAREST PAIR IN THE LEDGER AND THE LITERAL TRUTH OF IT.
# He changes how he fights - he gets off, which is the largest decision a
# knight makes in an afternoon - and the side is still going backwards. The
# verb table hands out nothing for `adapted` in either direction, and nothing
# is correct: dismounting is not a thing that improves an army, it is a thing
# a man does because the ground took the choice away.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_on_the_broken_ground, which is rough terrain, prowess
# 10 and no side row at all, and is `entrenched`/`up` - a man who is better on
# bad ground than the enemy. This is a man whose ground has stopped carrying
# his horse while he loses.
#
# AND IT IS NOT A DROWNING. The mod does not kill in a phase event and mail in
# a foot of water is a fact about the afternoon, not an ending. See the loc
# header.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GUARDED and the gate does not check for it, so the
# grant is live. Ten stress, no prestige - nobody writes down the hour a
# knight walked.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_he_leaves_the_horse_in_the_shallows = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_field_is_coastal_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Getting off is easier to decide when there is no line left to be
		# seen from the saddle.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_side_is_outnumbered_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = brave
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_he_leaves_the_horse_in_the_shallows"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_he_leaves_the_horse_in_the_shallows_toast
				desc = bc_knight_he_leaves_the_horse_in_the_shallows_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Where the wages actually are. He rides in a free company, and it is winning.
#
# THE GATE IS A WALK TO THE COMMANDER, NOT A CHECK ON HIM. The company holds
# the government; a man riding in the company may be a courtier with no
# government of his own to test. Asking the captain is the certain question
# and the one this moment is actually about - it is the company's afternoon,
# not his. Guarded on the standard pattern, `exists` as a sibling and the READ
# inside a trigger_if that repeats it, same as
# bc_knight_nothing_is_coming_from_behind.
#
# `wild`/`up`, AND THIS IS WHAT THAT PAIR WAS FOR. The ledger's own comment
# calls it "a moment that grants momentum and blows the horses in the same
# breath", and a company's winning hour is that shape exactly: the enemy is
# going and the baggage is behind him, and no contract in Essos or Westeros
# has ever paid a rider as well as the enemy's own train does. He goes. It
# helps - the side gets a rung out of it - and bc_spent_horse is the bill,
# because horses ridden that way come back walking.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_nobody_pays_for_him, which is `gold < 0` on the knight
# himself while he LOSES. That is a man with no money. This is a man with a
# very clear idea of where money is.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE AND A GUARDED MOMENTUM RUNG. The gate does not check for the
# rung, so the grant is live.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT SACK A TOWN, may not take prisoners and may not have him
# find anything in particular. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_where_the_wages_are = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			scope:combat_side.side_commander = { bc_he_leads_a_free_company_trigger = yes }
		}
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man who can still catch them is a man who will try to.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = greedy
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = generous
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_where_the_wages_are"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_where_the_wages_are_toast
				desc = bc_knight_where_the_wages_are_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The men a king can afford. Facing a crown, and the side is losing.
#
# THE OTHER HALF OF THE RANK QUESTION, AND IT IS NOT A MIRROR. The commander's
# version is about a banner everybody is chasing. This one is about what is
# standing in front of a single man when the host with the crown behind it is
# the one going forward: household troops, which are the only soldiers in
# Westeros who do nothing else all year and whose kit was bought by somebody
# who never had to ask the price.
#
# `bloodied`/`down` AND bc_bloodied. He is not outfought by a scheme or worn
# down by ground - he is meeting better-equipped men one at a time and the
# arithmetic of that shows up on the side as casualties, which is exactly what
# bc_bloodied means.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_the_crown_on_the_field, which reads THIS commander's own title
# tier while losing and is a moment about being a target. It is not that man's
# knight either: this gate reads the enemy, and the two would only ever coincide
# for a king fighting a king, where they are still two different sentences about
# two different sides of the field.
#
# TEN STRESS, NO PRESTIGE, AND A GUARDED FALTERING RUNG. There is nothing
# creditable in being outspent, and the gate does not check for the rung, so
# the grant is live.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A GUARD, an order or a company, may not put the king in
# it, and may not kill anybody. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_men_a_king_can_afford = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_facing_a_crown_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Being outnumbered on top of it is how a man ends up meeting them
		# without anyone beside him.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			bc_side_is_outnumbered_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = brave
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_men_a_king_can_afford"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_the_men_a_king_can_afford_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_men_a_king_can_afford_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Nothing has changed for him. His mind is not his own, and the side is losing.
#
# `entrenched`/`down` AND bc_shieldwall, AND THE ARGUMENT IS ABOUT WHAT IS
# RUNNING OUT AROUND HIM. A line coming apart is not running out of men first,
# it is running out of the willingness of each man to be the one who stays
# while his neighbour goes. He is not drawing on that supply, because he never
# had the ordinary account. The men either side of him work out - without
# saying it and without liking it - that standing where he is standing is
# easier than standing anywhere else, and a line that stops moving backwards is
# precisely what bc_shieldwall is.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_holds_the_breach, WHICH IS prowess >= 10 AND LOSING.
# That man is buying time with skill. This one is not better at anything; the
# thing that ruins the rest of his life is, for about a quarter of an hour,
# not a disadvantage.
#
# IT IS ALSO NOT A HERO MOMENT AND MUST NOT READ AS ONE. No prestige. Ten
# stress, because whatever this is costs him afterwards, and a guarded
# faltering rung, because the side is still losing and the chronicle should
# say so. The gate reads a trait and the side row, never a rung, so the grant
# is live.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT GIVE HIM A SPEECH, may not have anybody admire him, and may
# not say what he is thinking - the whole point is that nobody on this field
# can. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_nothing_has_changed_for_him = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_his_mind_is_not_his_own_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# It reads as steadiness rather than as an absence when the man can
		# also fight.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 10
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_nothing_has_changed_for_him"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_nothing_has_changed_for_him_toast
				desc = bc_knight_nothing_has_changed_for_him_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The ground here was worth something. The county is rich, the side is losing.
#
# `adapted`/`down`, THE RAREST PAIR IN THE LEDGER. `adapted` is the mod's word
# for reading the field and changing what you are doing about it, and this is
# the smallest and most literal version of that: a man who has been fighting
# an open-field fight all afternoon works out, somewhere in the fourth hour of
# going backwards, that he is no longer in an open field. Rich country in this
# setting means built country, and built country has to be fought through
# differently. The verb table hands out nothing for it, which is right - the
# army is not braced, not bled and not chasing, it is just somewhere else.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads the county, the
# opener and the side row and never asks about a rung, so the grant is live.
#
# TEN STRESS. He is giving away the best ground anyone here has stood on, and
# the mod has one currency for that.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_it_is_his_own_country_underfoot. That template asks whether
# the county is inside HIS realm - a question about title and obligation, which
# fires the same on a barren holdfast as on the richest county in the Reach.
# This one never asks who owns anything and would fire identically on a county
# belonging to the man across the field. Ownership and worth are different
# questions and the mod has now asked both.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A HOLDING OR A VILLAGE, may not burn, sack or count
# anything, may not put a number on the damage and may not move a single thing
# on the map. The mod does not touch the map. It may notice what is around him
# and it may not change it. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_ground_here_was_worth_something = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_field_is_rich_country_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man who is still winning his own small part of it has the room to
		# look up and notice where he is standing.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = callous
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_ground_here_was_worth_something"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_the_ground_here_was_worth_something_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_ground_here_was_worth_something_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The orders have stopped arriving. Enemy commander faltering, side winning.
#
# `ordered`/`up` AND bc_pursuit, WHICH IS THE POINT. This is the one place in
# the mod where a knight's moment is caused by something happening a quarter of
# a mile away to a man he has never met. He cannot see the enemy commander and
# would not know him if he could; what he can see is the rank in front of him
# still doing a thing that stopped making sense some minutes ago, because
# nobody has come to tell it otherwise. A formation that presses into that is
# a formation pursuing, and bc_pursuit is exactly the right consequence.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads the enemy
# commander's rung and the side row and never asks about his own side's.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE. For being the man who moved on it first, which on a field is
# the whole of the difference between noticing and gaining.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_the_banner_answerable AND NOT
# bc_knight_the_name_across_the_field. Both of those ask WHO the enemy
# commander is - whether he turned up, whether men know his name. This asks how
# his day is going, and the knight in this template never learns the answer to
# either of theirs.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT LET HIM WORK OUT THE CAUSE, may not name or see the enemy
# commander, may not break the enemy line and may not kill anybody. What he has
# is a gap of a few yards and a few seconds. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_orders_have_stopped_arriving = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_foe_commander_is_faltering_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Seeing the gap and being able to be in it are two different
		# qualifications, and this moment needs the second one.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_orders_have_stopped_arriving"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_the_orders_have_stopped_arriving_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_orders_have_stopped_arriving_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The reach is wrong. The foe is from across the sea and the side is winning.
#
# `adapted`/`up`. This is the smallest possible unit of the same event the
# commander half gets wrong: one man, four exchanges, a solvable problem. He
# has spent his life measuring distance against one length of weapon and one
# shape of shield, both of them are different today, and the correction is
# something he makes with his feet before he could say what he corrected. The
# verb table gives nothing for it, which is right - nobody has been bled,
# braced or launched. A man has simply got better at this in the last minute.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads his own culture, the
# enemy commander's, and the side row, and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_something_is_always_there OR
# bc_knight_the_name_across_the_field. The first is about the enemy commander
# being better than his own and the second about his being famous; both are
# facts a man could be told in a tent. This one is a fact he can only learn at
# the length of an arm.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A PLACE, A PEOPLE OR A WEAPON, may not have him
# despise anybody, may not kill anybody and may not let him win the fight he is
# in - it ends with him even, not ahead. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_reach_is_wrong = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_foe_is_from_across_the_sea_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Solving it inside four exchanges is a thing only a man with the hands
		# for it gets to do; everybody else solves it afterwards.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_reach_is_wrong"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_the_reach_is_wrong_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_reach_is_wrong_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The light is going. It is winter, and the side is losing.
#
# `ordered`/`down`, THE PAIR THE LEDGER DESCRIBES AS A FORMATION THAT KEEPS ITS
# SHAPE WHILE GOING BACKWARDS. That is precisely this. A winter afternoon has a
# hard edge on it, everyone in the rank has worked out roughly where that edge
# is, and the arithmetic that follows is not about winning: it is that a line
# which holds until dark walks away and a line which breaks before dark gets
# ridden down in it. So the shape holds, and it holds for a reason that has
# nothing to do with courage. The verb table hands out nothing on this pair,
# which is right - bc_pursuit is for a formation going forwards.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads the season and the
# side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_stands_in_the_snow, which is `entrenched`/`up`, gated
# on prowess and winter TERRAIN, and is about a hard man being equal to the
# cold. This man is not being asked to be equal to anything. He is watching a
# clock that is made of daylight.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT END THE BATTLE, may not have anybody ordered to hold, may
# not kill anybody and may not make him brave about it. The holding here is
# arithmetic. See the loc header.

		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes

		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man who has done a winter campaign before knows what the hour costs
		# and does the sum without being told.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			has_trait = winter_soldier
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# These are the ones who stayed. His lord has another war, and they are winning.
#
# `adapted`/`up`. He is standing in a rank made of whoever was left after the
# other war took its pick, and it is working - not because these men are as
# good, but because he has spent the afternoon quietly revising what he expects
# from each of them and covering the difference. That revision is the moment.
# The verb table hands out nothing for it, which is right: nobody has been
# bled, braced or launched, a man has simply stopped waiting for the army he
# used to have.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads his lord's war list
# and the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THE WALK IS THE STANDING ONE AND IS UNAVOIDABLE HERE. `any_character_war`
# lists a man's OWN wars and a sworn sword has none, so the question has to be
# asked of his lord. The `exists` sits as a sibling and the READ is nested in a
# trigger_if that repeats it, exactly as in bc_knight_where_the_wages_are.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_shieldbrother_forged, which counts knights on the side and is
# about a bond being made. Nothing is made here. He is adjusting an estimate.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT LET HIM KNOW WHY. He has no idea there is a second war and
# the toast may not tell him. It may not name the other war, may not disparage
# the men beside him, may not kill anybody and may not make him the reason the
# side is winning. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_these_are_the_ones_who_stayed = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
		exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander
		trigger_if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:combat_side.side_commander }
			scope:combat_side.side_commander = { bc_he_has_another_war_trigger = yes }
		}
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Carrying the difference for the men either side of him is work, and
		# it is work of a kind that has to be done with the hands.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 10
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_these_are_the_ones_who_stayed"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_these_are_the_ones_who_stayed_toast
				desc = bc_knight_these_are_the_ones_who_stayed_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# `entrenched`/`up` AND bc_shieldwall. He is the one man along this stretch

# side of him are worth exactly what today's fighting is worth and no more. He
# knows it. He is standing where they are standing anyway, and the shape holds
# because of and not despite that - a rank that can see one of its own choosing
# not to be somewhere safer is a rank that stays put.

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He has been the man his faith says to be, and the side is losing.
#
# `ordered`/`down` AND DELIBERATELY NO MODIFIER. He is doing the thing exactly
# as he was taught it, in the order he was taught it, and it is not working.
# The formation keeps its shape while going backwards, which is what the pair
# means, and the verb table's `ordered` row is the one row that tests SWING and
# hands out bc_pursuit only on `up` - so this moment produces no army modifier
# at all. That is the point of choosing it. Virtue is not buying anything on
# this field today, and the ledger should not pretend otherwise.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads his virtues and the
# side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_the_white_cloak, which reads bc_is_kingsguard_trigger.
# That is membership of an order - what a man was admitted to. This reads what his own
# faith makes of his character, and a Kingsguard with a bad temper answers no
# to it while a hedge knight who has kept every promise he ever made answers
# yes.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A VIRTUE OR A VOW, may not name his faith or its god,
# may not put a septon or priest on the page, may not have him pray, may not
# have anybody thank him, may not kill anybody, and may not let the moment
# rescue anything. He is losing at the end of it. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_it_was_never_a_bargain = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_his_own_faith_counts_for_him_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Keeping the shape while it goes backwards is work, and a man who
		# cannot do the work is simply carried off with the rest.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = just
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_it_was_never_a_bargain"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_it_was_never_a_bargain_toast
				desc = bc_knight_it_was_never_a_bargain_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He was raised for the dark, and the side is winning.
#
# `adapted`/`up`. He spent the first hour of this doing what he has done in
# every room he has ever been in - looking for the angle, the seam, the one man
# whose removal changes the shape of the problem - and somewhere in the second
# hour he stopped, because a shieldwall does not have a seam and there is no
# room, only a line and the next step. What he is doing now is the plainest
# work there is, done properly. That is a reading and a change made on it,
# which is what the KIND means, and the verb table hands out nothing, which is
# right: an army is not braced or bled by one man putting down his habits.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads his education and
# the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THIS IS NOT ANY OF THE ORIGIN TEMPLATES. bc_knight_no_house_behind_him reads
# `is_lowborn`, bc_knight_twice_as_good the bastard trait,
# bc_knight_the_squires_best_day the squire trait, and
# bc_knight_a_hall_is_waiting_for_him his heir titles. All four are about where
# a man stands in his family. This is about what somebody trained him to do,
# and a trueborn second son taught by a poisoner answers no to the first three
# and yes to both the fourth and this one.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A POISON, A KNIFE IN THE DARK, A SPY OR A ROOM, may not
# have him remember a previous piece of work, may not have him consider or
# reject a treachery today, may not have anybody suspect him, and may not kill
# anybody. The moment is a habit being set down, not a habit being resisted.
# See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_there_is_no_seam_in_a_shieldwall = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_he_was_raised_for_the_dark_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Doing the plain work properly, for a whole afternoon, is a thing that
		# takes a body as well as a decision.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = deceitful
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = honest
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_there_is_no_seam_in_a_shieldwall"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_there_is_no_seam_in_a_shieldwall_toast
				desc = bc_knight_there_is_no_seam_in_a_shieldwall_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# One banner on the whole field, and it is going badly.
#
# `entrenched`/`down` AND bc_shieldwall. There is exactly one host here and he
# is standing in it, which means the reserve is not late and is not lost - it
# does not exist, and neither does the second lord who might have been sent for
# yesterday. He has worked that out, and the working-out does not produce a
# decision so much as it removes every alternative to the one he is already
# doing. So the shape gets tighter rather than looser as the day goes wrong,
# which is what the pair means and what the verb is for.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads the banner count
# and the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_side_is_outnumbered, which is a ratio. A single banner can
# outnumber the men opposite comfortably and still be the only thing on this
# field that is ever going to arrive.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_no_house_behind_him EITHER, which reads `is_lowborn` -
# a fact about his birth. This is a fact about the afternoon, and the
# best-connected knight in Westeros answers yes to it if his lord came alone.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME A HOUSE OR A LORD, may not have anybody look for help
# or send for it, may not have a rider arrive or fail to arrive - a rider that
# does not come is still a rider, and there is no one to send - may not kill
# anybody, and may not let him hope. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes

	}

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high

			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = calm

		}
	}

	effect = {

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He is carrying one, and the side is winning.
#
# `wild`/`up` AND bc_spent_horse. This is the pair the ledger describes as
# granting a rung and blowing the horses in the same breath, and that is
# precisely what happens: the blade goes forward faster than a line can be
# kept, so the line stops being a line and becomes a wedge behind one man, and
# the animals underneath it pay for the afternoon. It is a good hour and an
# expensive one, and the mod has always insisted that those are the same hour.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads what he has equipped
# and the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_twice_as_good OR bc_knight_no_house_behind_him. Those
# read the bastard trait and `is_lowborn` - what a man was born as. This reads
# what he was handed, and a bastard given his father's sword answers yes to
# both without either moment being the other.
#
# THIS IS NOT A TEMPLATE ABOUT PROWESS. The chance row weights it, because a
# man who cannot fight does not get an hour like this one out of any sword, but
# the gate is the object and not the arm - which is the whole reason the mod
# had to go and look at equipment at all.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE SWORD OR THE HOUSE, may not describe the blade -
# no ripples, no smoke, no colour, no edge that never dulls - may not have
# anybody recognise it, may not have him think about who held it before him,

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He has already done the thing this world does not forgive, and the side is
# winning.
#
# `measured`/`up` - the smallest cell in the census. Every other man in this
# rank is doing sums about tonight, and he finished his sums years ago and the
# answer has not moved since. There is no worst outcome left in front of him;
# the worst outcome is behind him and has his name on it, and everything a
# battle can do to a man is a smaller number than that. What that leaves is not
# courage and is not despair. It is a very steady man doing the work at the
# rate the work is arriving, on an afternoon when the work is going well.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads three traits and the
# side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THIS IS NOT THE FAITH PAIR FROM BATCH EIGHTY. bc_the_field_keeps_no_accounts
# and bc_knight_it_was_never_a_bargain read a doctrine adding up sins and
# virtues, and a doctrine changes its mind when a man changes faith. This is a
# thing the Seven, the old gods, the drowned god and every hedge knight who has
# never thought about a god agree about, and no conversion touches it.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE DEAD MAN OR SAY WHAT THEY WERE TO HIM, may not say
# how or when or why, may not have him remember it, may not have anybody on the
# field know, may not make him monstrous and may not make him tragic, and may

# He is in a rank for a man nobody has crowned, and the side is winning.
#
# `adapted`/`up`. The men around him joined for the usual reasons - their lord
# came, or their fathers did, or there was no version of staying home - and

# country is not given to a man, it is what a run of afternoons like this one

# what the work is, and he is doing it faster.

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# There is a child coming, and the side is winning.
#
# `entrenched`/`up` AND bc_shieldwall, and the modifier is the point rather
# than a decoration. Everything he does for the rest of the afternoon is going
# to be a half step more careful than it was yesterday. He does not leave the
# line. He does not take the opening that is probably an opening. He holds his
# shield where it is supposed to be for four hours instead of where it is
# comfortable, and every man on either side of him is safer for it without
# anybody working out why. That is what a shieldwall is made of and it is
# almost never made of courage.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads a pregnancy and the
# side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_a_hall_is_waiting_for_him or the succession templates,
# which are about what a man stands to get. Nothing is coming to him here. A
# thing is coming that will need him, which is the opposite arrangement.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME HER OR THE CHILD, may not guess the sex, may not have
# him picture either of them, may not mention a name he has chosen, may not
# have him promise anything, and may not have him afraid. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_a_half_step_more_careful = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_a_child_of_his_is_on_the_way_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Holding a shield where it ought to be for four hours is work, and
		# the men who can do it are the men who are good at this.

			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = content
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = reckless
		}
	}

	effect = {

		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He is here because of a document, and the side is winning.
#
# `measured`/`up` - the smallest cell in the census. He has worked out, over
# the course of an afternoon in which there was nothing else to think about
# between charges, the exact chain that put him in this county: his lord's name
# is beside another name on something neither of them wrote, and everything
# from the muster to the ford to this hedge follows from that. It does not
# offend him. It is simply the first time he has seen the whole shape of it,
# and the shape is winning today, which is the only part of it that is any of
# his business.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads two links and the
# side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_the_man_who_is_the_name or
# bc_knight_what_a_run_of_afternoons_gets_called. The first is a man under the
# head of his own house, which is blood. The second is a man who has worked out
# what the afternoon is making, which is a future. This one is a man who has
# worked out what put him on the field in the first place, which is a past, and
# a very short and very dry one.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME EITHER LORD OR EITHER REALM, may not have him resent
# it, may not have him approve of it, may not mention a wedding or a hostage or
# a sum of money, and may not have him think about going home. See the loc
# header.

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# This county belongs to the men killing him, and the side is losing.
#
# `entrenched`/`down` AND bc_shieldwall, and the grant is the argument rather
# than a flourish. Every hedge, ford, drove road and dry crossing within a
# day's walk belongs to the people on the other side in the only sense that
# matters this afternoon, which is that they know where it is. There is
# therefore nowhere to go. A man who runs in a country that is not his runs
# into it, not out of it, and every man along this stretch has worked that out
# without discussing it, which is why the line is still a line at all.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads the county holder
# against the far commander and the side row, and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
