# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Nobody pointed him at this. He walked back to it late, and it is going badly.
#
# `bloodied`/`down`. He chose this. Some year in his twenties, with no tutor
# and nobody's blessing, he decided that this was the thing he was going to be
# and set about teaching himself out of other men's afternoons. It has taken.
# He is here, in the line, with a knight's place in it. What he does not have
# is the seven-year-old's fifteen thousand repetitions, and there is a specific
# hour in a losing fight when that stops being a thing he can cover with will
# and starts being a thing that is measured on his body - a parry that arrives
# correct and a quarter-beat late, a shield that comes back up from the wrong
# place because it was never put down from the right one. The verb table hands
# out bc_bloodied, which is the honest reading: the cost is going onto the
# army's account through him.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads an education, a
# lifestyle and the side row, and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_there_is_no_seam_in_a_shieldwall. That one requires an
# INTRIGUE education specifically and is on the winning side at `adapted`/`up`,
# and its argument is a man setting a lifetime of habits DOWN because they do
# not apply here. This is a man who picked his habits up on purpose, twenty
# years too late to get them free, and is paying the difference. Neither gate
# contains the other: that one takes any intrigue-raised knight however he
# spends his life, this one takes the learning-, stewardship- and
# diplomacy-raised as well and then narrows all four by a row that one never
# had.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_the_squires_best_day OR bc_knight_twice_as_good. Those
# read a trait about who he was born or apprenticed as. This reads a
# disagreement between two things the engine holds about the same man, and a
# trueborn merchant's son who took up the sword at thirty answers no to both of
# those and yes to this.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME AN AGE, A YEAR OR A NUMBER OF YEARS, may not name the
# trade he came from, may not have him regret the choice, may not have another
# knight sneer at him or notice him at all, may not have him think about who
# taught him, and may not kill him. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

	is_valid = {

			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man who taught himself all the way up to a knight's place is a
		# man who has spent a great deal of time thinking about exactly
		# which parts of it he does not have.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = impatient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_he_did_not_start_at_seven"

			}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# His place is an arrangement, and the man the arrangement is with is here.
#
# `adapted`/`down`. Everyone else along this stretch is defending something
# that stays where it is - a valley, a name, a roof their people are under. He
# is defending an agreement. If it goes badly enough today he does not lose
# land, because he has none to lose; he loses the rooms, the standing, the
# place at the table and the reason anybody has for remembering his name, and
# all four of those things are attached to one specific man who is currently
# sitting a horse two hundred yards behind him. So somewhere in the second half
# of the afternoon what he is actually doing quietly changes. He stops fighting
# for the field, which is not his, and starts fighting for the man, who is. It
# is a re-reading of what the day is about rather than a thing the day did to
# the army, which is what the KIND means, and the verb table hands out no
# modifier, which is right: one man privately deciding what he is here for does
# not brace a line or bleed it.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads a court position
# and the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_where_the_wages_are. That one requires the side
# commander to lead a FREE COMPANY, is on the winning side, and is `wild`/`up`:
# a man in a body of hired men going forward hard because forward is where the
# money is. This is a household man in an ordinary host, losing, and the point
# is that his arrangement is with one person rather than with a contract.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_a_hall_is_waiting_for_him, WHICH IS ITS EXACT OPPOSITE.
# That reads heir titles: a man who could be elsewhere and is not. This one is
# a man for whom there is no elsewhere, because the elsewhere was always the
# same hall he is fighting to keep a place in.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_no_house_behind_him. `is_lowborn` is about birth and a
# court position is about a contract, and a third son of a great house who took
# a post because there was nothing else for him answers no to that one and yes
# to this.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME AN OFFICE - no champion, no huntsman, no bodyguard, no
# master-at-arms - may not name a sum or a wage, may not have him look at the
# commander or be looked at, may not have him think about being dismissed in so
# many words, may not have him resent the arrangement, and may not have him
# swear anything. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_place_is_an_arrangement = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_the_man_who_pays_him_commands_here_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man good enough at this to be worth a post is a man who has
		# thought carefully about what the post is worth.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = content
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_place_is_an_arrangement"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_the_place_is_an_arrangement_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_place_is_an_arrangement_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He has already seen what one death does to a household, and it is going
# badly.
#
# `ordered`/`down`. He buried one wife and then, because that is what men in
# his position do, he went and built the whole apparatus again - a contract, a
# household, people who eat because he keeps eating. The difference between him
# and the man beside him is that he has watched the machinery of a family stop
# from the inside and knows exactly which parts seize first. So on an afternoon
# that is coming apart he does the least romantic thing available: he gives
# ground in step, keeps the man on either side of him, and declines every
# chance to make the day mean something. The formation goes backwards and does
# not break, which is what the KIND is for, and the SWING is why the verb table
# hands out nothing - a pursuit modifier for a rank walking backwards would be
# a lie about the afternoon.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads a marriage row, a
# burial row and the side row, and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_the_man_who_is_the_name. That one is a knight beside
# the head of his house and is about rank inside a family. This is a man's
# second household and the specific education a first one gave him, and a
# landless hedge knight twice married answers no to that gate and yes to this.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_a_hall_is_waiting_for_him. Titles waiting for a man are
# a reason he could leave; a household depending on him is a reason he cannot
# afford a gesture. Those are different sentences and the second one keeps him
# in the line rather than explaining why he is in it.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME EITHER WIFE, may not say how the first one died, may
# not put a child on the page, may not have him picture home, may not have him
# make a promise, may not have him be afraid, and may not let anybody else on
# the field notice what he is doing or thank him for it. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_he_has_seen_a_household_stop = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_he_buried_her_and_filled_the_place_trigger = yes

	}

			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high

		# and it is the same skill that keeps him alive doing it.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = reckless
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_he_has_seen_a_household_stop"

		add_stress = 10

			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_he_has_seen_a_household_stop_toast
				desc = bc_knight_he_has_seen_a_household_stop_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He took this man's terms once. He is about to find out what they cost twice.
#
# `bloodied`/`down`. The paper is his: he is the one bound by it, which means
# there was a war, and he was on the wrong end of it, and he sat somewhere with
# a dry mouth while the terms were read out and then agreed to them. Everyone
# else on this stretch is guessing about the man on the far side. He is not
# guessing. He knows how that host moves in the last hour, what it does with a
# broken flank, whether it takes men alive and what it charges for them, and
# every one of those facts is arriving this afternoon in the same order it
# arrived the first time. That is not foreknowledge doing him any good. It is a
# bill he has seen before, itemised, being presented again, and the verb table
# hands out bc_bloodied because the cost is going onto the army's account
# through the men who are learning it for the first time.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads a truce and the
# side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_the_old_wound OR bc_knight_the_old_sword. Those read a
# wound and a weapon and are about a man who has been in battles. This is about a man who has been in THIS battle, against THIS host,
# and signed the ending of it.
#
# THE SAME GATE AS bc_the_seals_are_still_wet_enough AND A COMPLETELY DIFFERENT
# ARGUMENT. That moment is a winning commander deciding how far a won afternoon
# may be pushed when he will have to live beside the loser under the same
# seals. This is a losing knight recognising the shape of an afternoon he has
# already survived once. The two pools roll separately, so no field can produce
# both.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT DESCRIBE THE EARLIER BATTLE, may not name the war or the
# field, may not say what the terms were, may not have him ransomed or
# imprisoned on the page, may not have him meet or see the enemy commander, may
# not have him warn anybody, and may not have him be resigned to dying. See the
# loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_he_has_read_these_terms_before = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes

	}

	chance = {

			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Recognising a host's habits from inside a line is a fighting man's
		# skill and not a scholar's.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = cynical
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = trusting
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_he_has_read_these_terms_before"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_he_has_read_these_terms_before_toast
				desc = bc_knight_he_has_read_these_terms_before_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# They are not his people and he understands every word of it.
#
# `adapted`/`up`. The men opposite are of another country entirely and he can
# follow them as easily as he follows his own rank, which is an accident of
# which halls the two of them belong to and nothing he ever earned. For most of
# the day it is only strange. Then, some time after it turns his side's way, it
# stops being strange and starts being information: he hears the same
# instruction given twice, which means it was not obeyed the first time; he
# hears one name shouted in a register nobody uses for an order; he hears
# forty yards of men stop talking to the far side of the field and start
# talking to each other. Nobody beside him has any of that. So he moves a half
# pace before the line in front of him admits anything is wrong, and it is
# right, and it keeps being right. That is a reading and a change made on it,
# which is what the KIND means, and the verb table hands out nothing, because
# one man hearing better than his neighbours does not brace an army.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads a language, a
# culture and the side row, and never asks about a rung.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_there_is_no_seam_in_a_shieldwall, THE OTHER KNIGHT
# `adapted`/`up`. That is a man setting down the habits of an upbringing
# because they do not apply here. This is a man picking up something he never
# had a use for until this afternoon, and the two gates share no row.
#
# THE GATE'S CULTURE ROW IS THE POINT AND IS NOT DECORATION. Understanding a
# countryman is not a fact about anything; every second man on the field does
# it. Understanding people who are not your own, well enough to hear a change
# of register in them, is the whole moment, and the row is what makes this
# disjoint from bc_foe_is_countryman_trigger rather than a wider version of it.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT QUOTE ANYTHING ANYBODY SAYS, may not name a language or a
# people, may not have him tell anyone what he heard, may not have him be
# thanked or believed, may not have him take a prisoner, and may not have him
# feel sorry for the far side. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_he_understands_the_far_rank = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_he_can_hear_what_they_are_saying_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Acting on half a second of noise, correctly, in a line, is a
		# fighting man's skill and not a linguist's.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = trusting
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_he_understands_the_far_rank"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_he_understands_the_far_rank_toast
				desc = bc_knight_he_understands_the_far_rank_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The man on the far side taught him how to stand, and he is standing.
#
# `entrenched`/`down`. Ten years of his life were spent in that hall being made
# into this, and the single most repeated thing in all of it - the thing that
# got him hit with a stick at nine and that he has never once thought about
# since - was where the feet go and what the shield does when the day turns.
# The day has turned. Everything else he might do this afternoon is beyond him
# now, but that one piece of it is not, because it was put in below the level
# where fear reaches. So he braces, and the men on either side of him brace
# because he did, and the line holds a while longer than it had any business
# holding. bc_shieldwall comes from the verb table and it is the whole argument
# of the moment: the lesson is doing the work, in front of the man who gave it.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads a relation and the
# side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS. It is going badly and he is not being paid in anything but the
# knowledge that he did the one part correctly.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_there_is_no_seam_in_a_shieldwall OR ANY OTHER SHIELDWALL
# MOMENT. Those are about what a formation is. This is about where a particular
# man's version of it came from and who is watching it.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_the_squires_best_day. A squire is an apprenticeship in
# arms and a fostering is a childhood; the squire trait says who trained his
# hands and this relation says who raised him, and a boy fostered at eight and
# squired to somebody else at fourteen answers yes to both about two different
# men.
#
# THE OPPOSITE END OF bc_he_taught_that_retreat AND DELIBERATELY NOT ITS
# MIRROR. That moment is the teacher using the lesson against the pupil and
# earning a pursuit for it. This one is the pupil using the lesson correctly
# while losing anyway. The gates are disjoint by construction and the pools
# roll separately, so no field can produce both.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE GUARDIAN, may not stage the childhood as a scene,
# may not have him remember being struck, may not have the two of them see each
# other, may not have him want to be seen, may not have him hate the man, and
# may not have him forgive him either. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_the_lesson_holds_the_line = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_the_man_who_raised_him_commands_the_far_side_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A lesson that survives a bad afternoon is a lesson that went in
		# deep, and the men it went deepest into are the ones who were
		# good at it.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_lesson_holds_the_line"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_the_lesson_holds_the_line_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_lesson_holds_the_line_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He cannot be the one who left. He is a named defender, the side is losing.
#
# `adapted`/`down`, THE RAREST PAIR IN THE LEDGER. `adapted` is the mod's word
# for reading the field and changing what you are doing about it, and this is
# the version of it that has nothing to do with terrain: what he re-reads is
# not the ground but the question. He came out here to win a battle. Somewhere
# in the going-backwards he works out that the battle is not the thing being
# decided about him, and starts fighting for a different and much smaller
# outcome. The verb table hands out nothing, which is right - he is not braced,
# not bled and not chasing, he is doing arithmetic.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED. The gate reads the war link and
# the side row and never asks about a rung.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THE GATE IS THE POINT AND IT IS NARROW ON PURPOSE. Most knights in this mod
# are courtiers and hired swords who are in neither war list, and they answer
# no. The men this fires for are the ones whose own name went into the quarrel
# - a landed knight called in, a lord serving in somebody else's line - and the
# whole moment is the difference between those two kinds of man standing in the
# same rank on the same bad afternoon.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_where_the_wages_are OR bc_they_are_not_his_smallfolk.
# Those are about what a man is owed and who he owes it to. This one is about
# a man who is not owed anything by anybody, because the obligation runs the
# other way and has his name at the bottom of it.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_the_ground_here_was_worth_something. That man re-reads
# the country he is standing in. This one never looks at the ground; the thing
# he re-reads is what losing will actually cost him, which is a question the
# field cannot answer and the settlement can.
#
# THE OPPOSITE END OF bc_the_season_was_his_to_choose AND NOT ITS MIRROR. That
# moment is a man collecting on a decision made in a warm room a year ago. This
# one is a man discovering that somebody else's warm room is where his afternoon
# came from. The gates are disjoint and the pools roll separately, so no field
# produces both.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME WHAT HE STANDS TO LOSE, may not put a place, a title or
# a number on the page, may not have him blame his lord, may not have him
# consider leaving and heroically decline, and may not let anybody come and get
# him. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_he_cannot_be_the_one_who_left = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_the_war_came_to_him_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man with a name in the quarrel and the standing to keep it is
		# the man who has already worked out what the terms will say.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			stewardship >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = content
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_he_cannot_be_the_one_who_left"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_he_cannot_be_the_one_who_left_toast
				desc = bc_knight_he_cannot_be_the_one_who_left_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He would rather not be remembered. Somebody knows, and the side is winning.
#
# `measured`/`up` AND NOTHING HANDED OUT. He is doing the work properly and the
# army is being served exactly as well as it would be by a man with nothing to
# hide, which is the point: nothing about the line changes. What changes is
# where the credit lands, and there is no army modifier for that.
#
# THE MOMENTUM RUNG IS STILL GRANTED, GUARDED, INSIDE THE COMMANDER BLOCK. The
# rung is a fact about the side and not a compliment to anybody. The guard sits
# inside the block because bc_has_momentum_trigger reads its own root and this
# is_valid is rooted on the knight; bc_knight_for_a_friend's comment makes the
# same point.
#
# FIFTY PRESTIGE, AND THE MOD IS AWARE OF THE JOKE. He is being paid in the one
# currency he is trying not to accumulate. That is not an error in the seam -
# prestige is what a good afternoon does to a man whether he wants it or not,
# and wanting it not to is the entire moment.
#
# THE ARGUMENT IS EXACTLY INVERSE TO bc_knight_it_will_be_his_name AND THAT IS
# DELIBERATE. That knight does the work well while knowing the credit will go
# to a man he cannot stand, and the moment is the swallowing. This one does the
# work well and spends a small part of his attention making sure the credit
# goes anywhere else at all, because a name that gets written down gets read,
# and somewhere there is a man who knows the other thing about him. Same cell,
# opposite direction, and the two gates share nothing.
#
# THE KNOWER IS NOT ON THIS FIELD AND THE TEMPLATE MAY NOT PUT HIM ON IT. The
# gate says only that the list of people who could say it is not empty. Every
# reading in which the knower is standing nearby, or has sent something, or is
# about to arrive, is content the engine cannot support.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_the_chain_that_put_him_here, which is a man reading the
# arrangement that got him into the line. This one is entirely about what
# happens after the line, in rooms, to a name.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT SAY WHAT THE SECRET IS OR WHO KNOWS IT, may not have him
# picture the knower, may not have anybody notice him being careful, may not
# have him refuse an order or do the work badly, and may not have the day
# threaten to expose anything. Nothing is closing in. That is what makes it
# bearable and it is also what makes it permanent. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_he_would_rather_not_be_remembered = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_somebody_out_there_knows_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Knowing which parts of an afternoon get written down, and which
		# do not, is the same skill that let him keep the thing this long.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			intrigue >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_he_would_rather_not_be_remembered"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_he_would_rather_not_be_remembered_toast
				desc = bc_knight_he_would_rather_not_be_remembered_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Nothing here is owed. He is one of somebody else's great men, the man
# commanding him has no hold on him, and the side is losing.
#
# `entrenched`/`down` AND bc_shieldwall, WHICH IS THE ARGUMENT AND NOT A
# FLOURISH. He braces, and the bracing is not courage arriving - it is the
# single available answer to a question he has been turning over since the
# afternoon started going wrong, which is what a man in his position can afford
# to have happen next.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED, inside the commander block, for
# the reason bc_knight_for_a_friend's comment gives.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THE GATE'S NOT IS THE WHOLE SUBJECT. A powerful vassal standing in his own
# liege's line is doing what powerful vassals do. This one is in somebody
# else's line, under a man who cannot summon him, cannot revoke anything from
# him and will not be asked about him afterwards by anybody who matters. Every
# order he obeys today is obeyed by choice, which sounds like freedom and is
# in fact the opposite: nothing here is owed, so nothing here is covered, and
# the whole afternoon is being kept somewhere as an account of what he is like.
#
# SO THE ONE OUTCOME HE CANNOT HAVE IS A PLACE THAT BROKE WITH HIS NAME ON IT.
# Not because the man commanding could punish it. Because it would be repeated
# in halls where it would be repeated accurately, for twenty years, by men who
# were not here. He sets himself and stays set for a reason that has nothing to
# do with this field and everything to do with rooms he will be standing in
# next winter.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_the_leash_holds, which is also `entrenched`/`down`.
# That is a man held in place by a specific thing another man has on him. This
# one is held in place by nothing at all, which turns out to hold better.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_where_the_wages_are. He is not being paid and there is
# no contract; he outweighs most of the men who could offer him one.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME HIS REALM, his liege, his titles or any hall, may not
# have him look at the commander or think a word about him, may not have him
# consider leaving, may not have anybody thank him or notice, and may not let
# the holding save the afternoon. See the loc header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_nothing_here_is_owed = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_he_answers_to_somebody_else_entirely_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Standing still while it comes apart is a physical business before
		# it is anything else, and the men who can do it are the men who
		# could always do it.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = diligent
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = lazy
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_nothing_here_is_owed"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = entrenched SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_nothing_here_is_owed_toast
				desc = bc_knight_nothing_here_is_owed_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He is not fighting the battle any more. The one he loves is somewhere in this
# same line, and the side is losing.
#
# `adapted`/`down`, THE RAREST PAIR IN THE LEDGER, AND THE MOST LITERAL USE OF
# `adapted` IN THE KNIGHT FILE. He reads the field and changes what he is doing
# about it, and what he changes it to is not a tactic. The verb table hands out
# nothing, which is right: he is not braced, not bled and not chasing. He has
# simply stopped solving the afternoon and started solving one distance.
#
# THE FALTERING RUNG IS GRANTED AND GUARDED, inside the commander block, for
# the reason bc_knight_for_a_friend's comment gives.
#
# TEN STRESS.
#
# THE GATE POINTS INTO THE RANKS AND THAT IS NEW. Every other `has_relation_*`
# in this file is aimed at the side commander or the enemy's, and this one asks
# whether the person is anywhere in the same line - which is where a bond
# nobody wrote down would actually be found.
#
# THE MOD DOES NOT KNOW WHERE EITHER OF THEM IS STANDING and the template must
# not pretend it does. He has an idea. An idea is all a man in a line ever has
# about anything more than twenty feet away, and the whole moment runs on the
# gap between the idea and the fact.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_knight_for_a_friend. That gates on `has_relation_friend`
# toward the man commanding him and is about serving somebody well. This one is
# not about the commander at all, is not about service, and would be identical
# if the commander were a stranger, which he may well be.
#
# THIS IS NOT bc_side_has_kin_knight_trigger'S MOMENTS. Blood in the ranks is a
# fact he was born into and can name to a herald. This is on no document, was
# arranged by nobody, and could end without a seal being broken - and it is the
# one moving him.
#
# THE DESC MAY NOT NAME THE PERSON, give their sex, house or title, may not
# have him find them, see them, reach them, call out, or learn anything about
# how they are, may not have anybody else understand what he is doing, and may
# not let the afternoon end well for either of them on the page. See the loc
# header.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
bc_knight_one_distance_instead_of_a_battle = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_the_one_he_loves_is_in_this_line_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Working across a line that is coming apart, instead of standing
		# in your place in it, is a thing only a man who is good at this
		# survives long enough to keep doing.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = brave
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_one_distance_instead_of_a_battle"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_one_distance_instead_of_a_battle_toast
				desc = bc_knight_one_distance_instead_of_a_battle_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# He has signed against the man whose banner is over him, and that man's army
# is losing. There is no version of the next hour in which he is allowed to
# look like a man watching something he wanted arrive. So he holds his exact
# place, at his exact pace, doing nothing that could be described afterwards
# by anybody standing near him - which is what `ordered` and `down` are: shape
# kept all the way through a thing going backwards, and deliberately no
# modifier, because nothing about it changes how the line performs.
bc_knight_he_cannot_be_seen_to_want_this = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_he_is_sworn_against_somebody_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Managing what a whole rank of men would say about you later,
		# while the rank is coming apart, is not a thing a straightforward
		# man is any good at.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			intrigue >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = content
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_he_cannot_be_seen_to_want_this"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_he_cannot_be_seen_to_want_this_toast
				desc = bc_knight_he_cannot_be_seen_to_want_this_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# The man commanding the far side has a lawful reason to hold him, and this
# side is losing, so the ordinary exit from a lost battle is closed. Yielding
# does not buy him a tent and a price; it buys him a room with a door. A man in
# that position stops fighting to survive the day and starts fighting so that
# nobody standing near him can afford to reach for him, which costs everyone
# involved a great deal of blood - `bloodied` and `down`, the line paying for
# one man's arithmetic.
bc_knight_there_is_no_ransom_in_it_for_him = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_the_far_side_could_take_him_lawfully_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Making yourself too expensive to collect, rather than merely
		# dangerous, is a specific piece of work and takes a specific
		# amount of skill to keep up for an hour.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_there_is_no_ransom_in_it_for_him"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_there_is_no_ransom_in_it_for_him_toast
				desc = bc_knight_there_is_no_ransom_in_it_for_him_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# people who trust him, and none of it is any use in a rank that is coming
# apart. `bloodied`/`down` is the honest consequence: the only tools he has
# left here are the ones he has practised least.
bc_knight_none_of_his_arrangements_reach_this_far = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_the_years_are_going_into_the_dark_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# A man whose whole practice is arranging for things to happen
		# without him being in the room has an unusually bad afternoon
		# when the room is a field and everybody can see him.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			intrigue >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = paranoid
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = brave
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_none_of_his_arrangements_reach_this_far"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_none_of_his_arrangements_reach_this_far_toast
				desc = bc_knight_none_of_his_arrangements_reach_this_far_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# --- the quarrel opposite is with somebody else -------------------------------
#
# GATE. bc_the_name_opposite_is_half_of_an_old_argument_trigger, read on the
# enemy commander and carrying the exact negation of the commander-side gate,
# so a field with one of these names at each end produces the commander page
# only. The knight is losing.
#
# WHAT THIS MAY NOT SAY. Same prohibition as its pair and one more: the gate
# establishes that the far commander's name means something old, and expressly
# not that this knight is the other half of it. So this page may not claim the
# quarrel is his, may not name a house, a sigil, a seat or a river, and may not
# narrate the origin. What it may notice is that the men opposite are fighting
# him the way you fight a stranger, briskly and without interest, because
# whatever they are actually angry about is not standing in front of them.
#
# KIND. ordered/down. He is going backwards without coming apart, which is the
# one pair the verb table deliberately leaves empty; the rung and the stress are
# the whole consequence.
#
# SKILL. prowess, because noticing how you are being fought is a thing you learn
# from the inside of a melee rather than from a map.
bc_knight_their_quarrel_is_with_somebody_else = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_the_name_opposite_is_half_of_an_old_argument_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_losing_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# You have to have been hated properly at least once to be able
		# to tell, from the way a man comes at you, that he is not
		# bothering.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = vengeful
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = calm
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_their_quarrel_is_with_somebody_else"

		add_stress = 10

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_is_faltering_trigger = yes } }
				bc_faltering_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = down }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_bad
				title = bc_knight_their_quarrel_is_with_somebody_else_toast
				desc = bc_knight_their_quarrel_is_with_somebody_else_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# --- three lines of a letter, eventually --------------------------------------
#
# GATE. bc_there_is_a_chain_and_a_tower_behind_him_trigger, read on the knight
# himself. He keeps a maester, which means he is a landed man who is out here
# doing this in person anyway. He is winning.
#
# THIS IS NOT THE STATION AXIS. bc_the_crown_on_the_field and
# bc_nothing_but_the_horse read title tier and `is_landed` on a COMMANDER, and
# no knight template in the mod has ever read tenure at all. This one does not
# read it either: it reads a household expense, which happens to imply tenure
# the way a stable implies a horse.
#
# KIND. measured/up. Nothing is being done to his army; he is winning and
# noticing something about the shape of the record, which is exactly the read
# the verb table is built to leave alone.
#
# WHAT THIS MAY NOT DO. It may not have him kill a named man, may not have him
# reach any commander, may not put the maester on the field or in his thoughts
# as a voice, and may not end the day.
#
# SKILL. stewardship, because a man who runs a holding and rides in the line is
# the man who would think about the ledger in the middle of the afternoon.
bc_knight_three_lines_of_a_letter_eventually = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_there_is_a_chain_and_a_tower_behind_him_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# The habit of thinking about how a thing will be written down
		# afterwards belongs to men who have spent years signing what
		# somebody else wrote.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			stewardship >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = impatient
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_three_lines_of_a_letter_eventually"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = measured SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_three_lines_of_a_letter_eventually_toast
				desc = bc_knight_three_lines_of_a_letter_eventually_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

# is the whole point of calling it wild rather than ordered: he is doing well
# and he is using the animal up doing it, and the reason he keeps going forward
# is a thought rather than an order.
#
# WHAT THIS MAY NOT DO. It may not reach the far commander, see him, kill him,
# kill a named man at all, name the other party to the match, have anybody speak
# the arrangement aloud, or end the day. It may not be told to stop.

# --- the most dangerous thing in his life is not on this field ----------------

# GATE. bc_somebody_is_trying_to_have_him_killed_trigger, read on the knight

# KIND. wild/down, so this takes bc_spent_horse. He is on the wrong end of the
# afternoon and using the animal up staying in it, which is exactly what the

# today. The page carries the knowledge and he does not, and it may not name

# --- they were beaten before he got here --------------------------------------
#
# GATE. bc_the_host_opposite_is_sick_trigger, read on the enemy commander and
# carrying the exact negation of the commander-side gate. The knight is winning.
#
# KIND. adapted/up. Nothing is being done to his army; he is revising upward
# what he thought this afternoon was, which is the read the verb table exists to
# leave alone. There is no army modifier that means "he worked out why they are
# slow", and there should not be.
#
# WHAT HE MAY NOTICE AND WHAT HE MAY NOT. He may notice a host that moves wrong
# from fifty paces - slow, short-ranked, sitting down when it should not be. He
# may not name the sickness, know where it came from, be told anything, catch
# anything, reach the far commander, kill a named man, or end the day. He may
# not pity them out loud and he may not gloat to anybody.
#
# SKILL. learning, because reading a body of men as ill rather than as merely
# beaten is a piece of knowledge and not a piece of nerve.
bc_knight_they_were_beaten_before_he_got_here = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_the_host_opposite_is_sick_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Most men in a winning line see a beaten enemy. Seeing an ill
		# one takes having been somewhere with a sickness in it and
		# having paid attention while you were there.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			learning >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = compassionate
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_they_were_beaten_before_he_got_here"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = adapted SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_they_were_beaten_before_he_got_here_toast
				desc = bc_knight_they_were_beaten_before_he_got_here_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}


# --- winning this one does not finish anything --------------------------------
#
# GATE. bc_the_man_opposite_has_a_second_host_trigger, read on the enemy
# commander and carrying the exact negation of the commander-side gate. The
# knight is winning.
#
# KIND. bloodied/up, so it takes bc_bloodied on a winning side. That pairing is
# already shipped in twenty-two other places and it is the right one here: the
# rank is going forward and it is being taken apart while it does, and the point
# of the moment is that the price is being paid for something that settles less
# than the men shouting believe.
#
# WHAT THIS MAY NOT DO. It may not say where the other host is, who leads it,
# what it is doing, or when it will come. It may not have a rider arrive with
# news, may not have anybody senior explain the war to him, may not reach the
# far commander, may not kill a named man, and may not end the day.
#
# SKILL. martial, because knowing that a broken host on a field is not the same
# thing as a beaten enemy is the one piece of strategy a man in a rank is
# actually in a position to have learned.
bc_knight_winning_this_one_finishes_nothing = {
	type = knight

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_the_man_opposite_has_a_second_host_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# The men who have been in more than one of these are the ones
		# who can tell the difference between the end of a battle and
		# the end of a war while the first one is still going on.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			martial >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = patient
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = arrogant
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes

# KIND. entrenched/up, so it takes bc_shieldwall, and the argument earns it. The

		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_the_line_runs_through_her_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Somebody who has never had to ask whose name the day goes under
		# spends none of it wondering and all of it on the work in front.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = reckless
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = calm
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_the_line_runs_through_her"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = wild SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_the_line_runs_through_her_toast
				desc = bc_knight_the_line_runs_through_her_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight

	type = knight

		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_he_follows_a_man_with_five_names_on_him_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Standing close to a man with that much behind him and still reading
		# him plainly is a talent for people rather than a talent for arms.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			diplomacy >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = calm
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = vengeful
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_he_follows_a_man_with_five_names_on_him"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = ordered SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_he_follows_a_man_with_five_names_on_him_toast
				desc = bc_knight_he_follows_a_man_with_five_names_on_him_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}

	is_valid = {
		is_alive = yes
		bc_chronicle_ready_trigger = yes
		bc_one_house_against_the_whole_gathering_trigger = yes
		bc_side_is_winning_trigger = yes
	}

	chance = {
		base = 80
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_low
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			has_game_rule = bc_frequency_high
		}
		# Being the only ones out here is either unbearable or clarifying,
		# and which it is depends almost entirely on the man.
		modifier = {
			add = 30
			prowess >= 12
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.5
			has_trait = stubborn
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			has_trait = craven
		}
	}

	effect = {
		save_scope_as = bc_knight
		bc_mark_chronicle_effect = yes
		debug_log = "BC: fired bc_knight_one_house_against_the_whole_gathering"

		add_prestige = 50

		scope:combat_side.side_commander ?= {
			bc_battle_open_effect = yes

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { bc_has_momentum_trigger = yes } }
				bc_momentum_minor_effect = yes
			}

			bc_record_moment_effect = { TIER = minor KIND = bloodied SWING = up }

			send_interface_toast = {
				type = event_toast_effect_good
				title = bc_knight_one_house_against_the_whole_gathering_toast
				desc = bc_knight_one_house_against_the_whole_gathering_toast_desc
				left_icon = scope:bc_knight
			}
		}
	}
}
